ACW — Pay-per-view

End Game 2010, 31 December 2010

31 December 2010
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
A C W THE EMPIRE STRIKES BACK END GAME 2010 ¤ 31 DECEMBER 2010 STADIO OLIMPICO, ROME, ITALY INTRODUCTION ¦ PRE-SHOW HOUR ONE ¦ HOUR TWO ¦ HOUR THREE ¦ HOUR FOUR Introduction If we open a quarrel between the past and the present, we shall find that we have lost the future. -- Winston Churchill There is good. And evil. Red and black. Life and death. The joke and the punchline, if you will. This is the big one. Throughout ACW's existence in the modern era of television and increased global recognition, which kicked off in earnest circa September 2002 thanks to the work of one Mr. Ethan Winters, there have been numerous assaults on the federation's livelihood by a wide array of individuals. But now, more than ever, everybody in ACW feels that a massive shift is on the verge of occuring. Something primal and fundamental is about to happen. Scary, isn't it? When you can see what's coming and yet you can't do anything about it. Some very, very bad people have decided to hurt ACW. Hurt it so bad, it'll never recover. Why? Because they can. Because they think that the power-that-be at this moment in time, the one and only SilverHAWK, deserves it. Vengeance is a powerful motivator. It inspires the worst in people. ... And tonight, the quest for vengeance will either be fully realised and fulfilled, or it will be snuffed out by the man who's brought ACW to its apex. SilverHAWK and his chosen savior. Jeremy Hunt and God's Forgotten Son. THE BATTLE FOR ACW. Khristain Keller. Keith Scott Zimmerman. ACW World Championship on the line. 30 All-Stars. 4 rewards. 1 ultimate winner with the biggest prize ever. 2010 End Game Match. Ryan Billows. Midnight Cowboy. One man's end, literally. Rud Albion. Dean Denton. Respect shall be had. Arkady Rasputin. Maddox St. James. The Pack needs a definite leader, and it will have one. Abilities. Two giant behemoths that're part of a rebel tribe. The Night Life. Four Ways To Glory, and one path to the ACW Tag Team Titles. Andy Sharp. Omega. A titanic clash of two gigantic personalities. And amid all of that, a masked enigma will usher in the new year with the end of his doomsday clock... Strap in for the ride of your lives. Get ready for the end of the year, and the start of a fresh year of mayhem. The beginning of... a brave new world. Next: PRE-SHOW. ---- IN THE DARK~! IN THE DARK~! #1: Saleem Nagi vs. Spike Saunders Even with the insanely stacked card in store for the throngs of ACW's fans purchasing the show with their hard-earned money, it seemed as if ACW wasn't about to let the live crowd packed within the Stadio Olimpico -- those that had come in early -- stand around doing nothing. Oh, no. There was a very special surprise for them; the first-ever IN THE DARK~! match-up on a pay-per-view. And this one was a big one, at that. Having been victorious on WORLD.WIDE.WRESTLECIDE 005 several weeks ago (you should ready the transcript of that if you haven't), Saleem Nagi had earned an opportunity to earn a spot in the highly-anticipated 2010 End Game Match. Saleem, a newcomer who had previously rocked audiences in his previous employ back in the States, had to do battle with the man who ended up being his tag team partner all those weeks ago. And that was none other than the Colossus himself, Spike Saunders. Yes, Saunders was in the house, having shockingly returned from the wilderness at WORLD.WIDE.WRESTLECIDE. And Saunders looked in fantastic shape. He had a great chance to put a cap on his comeback to ACW, and the former ACW Tag Team Champion was also presumably in attendance to lock horns with PowerTrip at some point in the pay-per-view. Despite that looming ahead for Saunders, the Colossus didn't take the ensuing battle with Saleem like it was a warm-up contest. The two men shook hands prior to the ringing of the bell, which made referee Leon Hurst's life a hell of a lot easier. From there, Spike and Saleem gave it everything they had. Saleem's unique offensive style was just about enough of a match for Spike's power brawling and massive size advantage. What the fans got was a hell of a fight, with both men eager to win that spot in the End Game Match. In the end, it was the wily veteran Spike Saunders that triumphed after about 10 and a half minutes of non-stop action. Saleem found himself outmatched at the end, and Spike's punishing DreamKiller finisher was something Nagi could not get out of. Following the bell, both men shook hands again, much to the crowd's delight. A great show of sportsmanship, prior to a pay-per-view which promised nothing but matches overflowing with animosity. Spike grabbed a microphone before he left the ringside area, and urged everybody that he had a trick up his sleeveless sleeve that was guaranteed to stun PowerTrip later on. That sounded like a lot of fun to the fans. As for Saleem Nagi? So close yet so far. The promising newcomer could only reflect on what had been a glorious opportunity. Perhaps next time, Saleem. Perhaps next time. nightfall - 1 It was a cold night. Not unlike any other night in December, in Rome. And outside the Stadio Olimpico, two people stood out from amongst the crowd filtering into the building. These two individuals weren't Romans. They were in fact scheming bastards. One was a legend, and the other was a dastardly mastermind. They ignored the hateful staring aimed at their direction and quickly veered off the path, heading towards one of the makeshift service entrance/exit doors. "Ah, here we are then. Destiny's calling, my avatar." 'The One' Jimmy Reid quipped as he placed his hands into the side pockets of his swank leather jacket and turned to his companion. Z looked back at Jimmy somewhat quizzically. "Destiny's calling? It's already arrived, I should say. Tonight, I go for the juggular. Tonight, we transcend to the next level. We're still missing one important piece, though. Have your sources dredged up anything about John Sarsgaard? His role in this expires at the end of tonight, and I'd rather dovetail onto what he has rather than fall back on Plan B. I'm not keen on Plan B." "OH REALLY?" Jimmy retorted jokingly, guffawing for a moment. "You slay me, Z. Plan B is a masterstroke. And you're most definitely going to have to resort to that. Sarsgaard's fallen off the grid, again. Perhaps you shouldn't have put the fear of the Almighty Christ in him, hmmm? Heh heh." The Caped Crusader looked down at the ground and tried his hardest to hide his growing smile. Jimmy chortled and took a deep breath, soaking in the silent moment. These two men, unknown to most, had been comrades in cahoots for a long time. A bond had been built. In some ways, their relationship -- exposed to the general public on COURAGE 130 -- had been the only thing keeping Jimmy Reid from going completely mental. But that's a different story for another day. "You're coming in now, then?" Jimmy finally spoke up, breaking the silence. Z cleared his throat. "Nah. I'm going to stay out here for a while." Z responded, his gaze meeting Jimmy's. Reid shrugged his shoulders while flashing a crooked smile, suggesting he knew what the Masked Enigma was up to. Without another word spoken between the two, Jimmy turned on his heels and walked towards the door to the service entrance. In a flash, he was gone. Z turned and set his eyes on the camera-man. Shaking his head, Z dug into one of his many pockets of his patchwork trenchcoat. Most probably hunting for a pack of cigarettes, or a condom, or something weird. He's Z, after all. Before he could produce whatever he was looking for, however, Z found himself catching on to the camera-man backing up a bit. The Caped Crusader didn't know it, but there were several people coming out from the shadows behind him. "What? Why are you stepping away from me?" Z asked, confusion washing over him. And then, he finally caught on. Z quickly swivelled on the balls of his feet and came mask-to-face with several rather familiar individuals. Rory Hayes. Ulf German. Chris Moliano. "Hh." Vintage Z, wouldn't you say? "Surprised to see us?" Rory spoke up first, with Ulf scowling at the Caped Crusader and just about ready to charge forth at the diabolical monster that put him through all sorts of hell a while back. Z placed his gloved hands together and yawned. "Not really." "Our group is incomplete." Chris Moliano suddenly stated, catching Z's attention who immediately dropped his arms by the side of his body. Stepping out from the darkness, was the fourth man. The man Z was just speaking about. "Tonight, destiny fails you!" John Sarsgaard roared at the top of his voice, face contorted into a mask of pure rage and vindiction. Immediately, Rory Hayes and Ulf German advanced on Z, tackling the Dastardly Mastermind to the ground. Chris Moliano headed in the opposite direction, towards the service entrance door. John Sarsgaard walked over to the camera-man, sweating profusely. The Hands Of Steel had a crazed look in his eyes, but there was a twisted grin on his face. Probably because of Ulf and Rory kicking the holy shit out of Z. "You may be recording this for the DVD and not for the live crowd, but I have this to say!" Sarsgaard shouted, right hand clenched tightly. "The resistance to that masked man's craziness starts here. I take responsibility for putting an end to his doomsday clock! I know what he needs to get to the last step, and it's my shot at the ACW World Championship! He will not get it! Hell, he won't even get into the building. Consider this my new year's present to you! SCREW THE CABAL, SCREW Z! IT GRINDS TO A HALT TONIGHT!" Well, then. This night hadn't even begun in earnest, but already a twist of epic proportions had gone down. If only the live crowd packed into the Stadio Olimpico could see this. Immense. ... What to make of Z slowly rising behind Sarsgaard, then? "You wish." John turned around, growling. Oh, it. WAS. ON. We'll find out through the course of the evening. Promise. Next: HOUR ONE. ---- Kick-off! Fireworks. Whoooooo. A shitload of 'em. Mix that with a LOT of screaming fans with signs, and beverages, and snacks, and programme sheets, and all that jazz. What do you have? An opening to All-Star Championship Wrestling's biggest pay-per-view to date. And once the fireworks ended, the true mark of the crowd's enthusiasm could be heard. And it was immense. Man alive! Everybody had their sights on the squared circle set in the middle of the Stado Olimpico, the chosen site for END GAME 2010. As for the people watching everything unfold on their television sets or on their computer monitors, the attention fell on the ringside area. They caught a glimpse of the Pakistani Broadcast Booth, the newly-formed Italian Broadcast Booth, and then the standard Broadcast Booth. You know, the dynamic duo who were the voices of ACW. Although, with Jimmy Reid's shocking defection to the cabal... yeah, that just left Renaud Cardinal, ACW's premire play-by-play commentator. But look! He had a new partner with him already. Let's throw it to Renaud and his new partner, shall we? RENAUD: For all the fans of All-Star Championship Wrestling watching at home; welcome to END GAME 2010! I'm Renaud Cardinal, your play-by-play man for the evening. It will be my EXTREME pleasure to navigate you through what should be an extraordinary evening of highs and lows! And my partner for night is none other than... Jenna McMullen! JENNA: Hiiiiiii, everybody! Ohmygosh, I'm so gosh-darn excited, I might just pee. But, yes! WELCOME TO END GAME 2010! Tonight promises to be superb, unless the bad guys win everything. But I, like many others, have faith that the good guys will prevail and the bad guys will devail! RENAUD: I don't know if the word 'devail' actually exists, but it's good to have you sitting in this chair beside me, Jenna. Let's just hope you don't end up like the man who used to occupy that chair. A man I respected, a man that many fans and crew respected, until he revealed his true colours. A turncoat! JENNA: Never! Never will I join Z and his dastardly gang! I used to like him, since he had that 'bad boy' thing going. But I've soured on him. Anyways, maybe we should move on to the pay-per-view itself? We have a long night ahead of us. RENAUD: Yes, we do. Excellent point, Jenna. We have a LOT to talk about, fans, but we will have to save the hype for the rest of the night for later. At least, certainly after our first match of the evening. Which is... THE BEGINNING OF THE END! MATCH, that is. Ha! JENNA: Oh, I'm so excited! Let's get right to it! Yay! So it came to be. The replacement for Jimmy Reid's position at the Broadcast Booth was Jenna McMullen, ACW's premiere backstage interviewer. At least for the night. Interesting choice. Right. Time to get the show rolling, as it were. Now last year this was the time that SilverHAWK, le General Manager, would make his immeasurable presence known so those in attendance could all be made readily aware of one thing -- the End Game Match, and most specifically, how it would start. Yeah, we're talking about the Beginning Of The End Match. It’s happened twice so far, deciding the first and last spots in the BIG match itself. The unlucky #1 spot, and lucky #30. So far, the men who had won the Beginning Of The End Match had gone on to last to the final three in the respective End Game Match… and then proceeded to win both the United States Championship (Christopher Sheffield) and Spirit Of ACW Championship (Midnight Cowboy), respectively. Tonight was a night that so much was up for grabs… not only in the End Game Match itself, and every major Championship within the federation, but also the very future of All-Star Championship Wrestling. It was the final roll of the dice for the fate of All-Star Championship, with Jeremy Hunt and SilverHAWK as the generals that had led the charge for so many months now. SilverHAWK, several weeks ago, had fallen in battle… the extent of which, still wasn’t certain but it surely didn’t look good at all. ACW was on the brink of a new, very dark, age… but all was not lost as the certain Champion put forward by the Huntsmen would be none other then the Spirit of ACW, the man responsible not only for the BATTLE itself, but for what had happened to SilverHAWK. Ethan Winters Jr. -- God’s Forgotten Son. He was intent on destroying those that had brought down his father. So it was fitting that the man that held a shot at the Spirit Of ACW was none other then the man who had saved ACW from Winters Inc. on that fateful night of the first LEGENDS pay-per-view. Christopher Sheffield was ready to fight for SilverHAWK… for ACW. After all that the Huntsmen had done to him, to his federation. But that fateful match-up, and the many more to come before the end of 2010, not to mention four new All-Stars from throughout the history of ACW being inducted into the ACW Hall of Fame, could not begin without one simple match-up. The Beginning Of The End Match. And it had the potential of including any of the thirty competitors within the End Game Match. Yes, even the surprise entrants. So without much further ado was Tommy Vale with the announcement. ACW’s esteemed ring announcer, celebrating New Year’s Eve in Italy just like everyone else tonight, raised the envelope that he held in his hands. Grinning like the sexy beast that he was, Vale kicked off the festitivies. “Ladies and gentlemen! Welcome to END GAME 2010. Let's get right to it, shall we?" Vale started, drawing a huge round of cheers. "The two competitors in tonight’s Beginning Of The End Match wiiill be as follows.” Tommy Vale then popped the red wax off of the back of the crisp white envelope and brought out the END GAME 2010 stamped card. The anticipation was palpable. Who was the first competitor? Vale cleared his throat and opened his mouth: “FEJONA MIN!” The crowd jeered, a heavy amount of loathing in the air for the Female Buzzsaw of the cabal who had haunted one Christopher Sheffield these last several weeks. The jeers where replaced by a collective gasp as all in attendance waited to hear who she had been randomly matched with. “And her opponent?” Vale paused for dramatic effect, his eyes widened for a moment as he shook his head in disbelief. You wouldn't quite believe it. “CHRISTOPHER SHEFFIELD!” Talk about an explosive way to kick off an explosive pay-per-view, huh? THE BEGINNING OF THE END MATCH - STANDARD RULES Fejona Min vs. Christopher Sheffield REFEREE: LI MU BAI The fans cheered loudly, but there was a smattering of disbelieving grunts from several sections of the crowd. If Sheffield was fighting the woman that had made his life a living hell these last several months, which would be quite the match to be sure, then what did this mean for... ZE BATTLE FOR ACW? The Original Pulp Hero was shown for a moment, fuming in his locker room, he was almost certain this must have been fixed that someone in the cabal… in their new understanding with the Huntsmen was looking to take him out of play, just as they had done to SilverHAWK at the end of COURAGE 130. The cameras then cut to Fejona Min, who also seemed surprised by this sudden turn of events. Furious even, at first. She was shaking her head in disbelief, but then suddenly became quite pleased that she had been put in this position with Sheffield. Even if it could hurt her position within the End Game Match. The photograph hung in her mind, and she knew that whatever she had hoped to say to Sheffield by the time the End Game Match came around… it wouldn’t have to wait. In short, this was quite the fix-up. A blessing of sorts. Min stepped up to where the curtains separated her from the Olimpico and a cold look came across her face. Tonight, Lady Vengeance would begin the end of the Original Pulp Hero. Tonight, her ascension to the top would begin in earnest. Lucky You, by The Deftones. Three things instantly happened: a) Buttload of jeering. b) Add in a fair amount of vehement hissing. c) And top it off with the ring already filling up with trash. There, a neat summary of what welcomed Fejona Min as she walked out from the back. With a single nod of her head, the Rogue Slayer marched down to the ring. Min, brandishing a tiny band-aid above her eyebrow from the BATTLEFIELD that ended COURAGE 130, looked otherwise spectacular as normal. Rolling into the ring, Fejona glanced at the assigned referee of this match: Li Mu Bai. He held himself with a professional and confident air that Min was sure Z could break in due time, just as Z had done to the likes of Paige Allen and Trent Savage. The Femme Fatale, surprised herself by the ferocity of the Roman crowd's jeering, took her place in the corner of the ring and kept to herself. No point in being fancy when she was being crucified by the fans, she figured. They must have figured by now that the fix was on for the Heart And Soul of ACW, and what had happened wasn’t simply the random beauty of the universe bringing together two violent enemies. Just as the universe had taken Fejona away from ACW in 2004 to reveal secrets heretofore unknown to the young woman. Just as the universe had brought her back to ACW in 2010, eventually to Z’s side where the blurred picture in her mind could crystalize and become assured of the very information that had always been hidden from her. A tale of two paths. Two… well, Fejona was planning on getting to that. RUMBLE. But before she could, Link Wray’s guitar was giving her a more immediate concern, that of the Man Without an Alias. Christopher Sheffield tore down the entrance, and made a b-line for the ring. If he waned to high-five the fans in the front rows of the Stadio Olimpico it’d have to wait until later. Right now? He was dead set on getting the first [Black] Scorpion Champion offa his gaddamn back, so he could worry about more pressing matters. Not her mind games. Not her machinations. Not that nostalgic picture that he wasn’t able to free from his pant’s pocket. On his mind were the cabal, the Huntsmen, and what they were doing to ACW. DING DING DING! The two opponents circled each other like eagles, trying to analyze one another. The mannerisms, outward emotions, underlying anger... the two mirrored each other in a certain way. Violent urges mixed with finesse. Where there was power, it was replaced with speed. Both, it could be seen, were still effected by their IMPRESSIVE battles on COURAGE 130, but neither would let that affect them. They were ready and will do anything to one another, within the label of the law set by referee Li Mu Bai. You know, whatever the hell these two certifiable lethal weapons could use to their advantage. Finally, Fejona Min got sick of waiting and lashed out at the man responsible for the anger lying from such untold history buried within the photograph that she had given to Sheffield, with a right jab. However, Sheffield was quick to respond to that, and moved behind her, the momentum of the fellow former Scorpion Fighting and Television Champion catching her off balance. The Pulp Hero locked his arms around Min’s waist, setting up for a German Suplex. However, the Cantonese Killer Queen realized this, and quickly threw two wild kicks back, connecting with Sheffield’s right knee (hard), and causing the Original Pulp Hero to release the waistlock. “What is it?” Sheffield growled. “What’s your problem? You never felt appreciated for all that you’ve done?” “Appreciated?!” Fejona hissed back. “You mean, like YOU, Sheffield?” Evidently, and not surprisingly... nothing was going to be held back and emotions would of course come into play. If anything else, you just kneeew this shit was going to be violent. Sheffield stumbled off of Fejona, and the Female Buzzsaw quickly realized the weak spot of her opponent, her prey... grabbing his arm, and taking Sheff down into an armbar. However, the Man Without An Alias flipped onto his feet, and looked intently at his hyper-intuitive fellow challenger. Fejona Min happily kicked Sheffield in the face, rocking him backwards, but held stead-fast onto his arm. Another kick into the stomach allowed her to whip Sheff into the corner. With the Fejona Min right on his heels, Sheffield had a grand idea to leap over the top rope and onto the apron. Small problem, Fejona Min was a more agile competitor even with the beatings that Omega had given to her in the past, and hit her opponent square in the head with a springboard dropkick, knocking the near twenty-year veteran onto the floor. Without a moment's hesitation, the doe-eyed Fejona Min hit the ropes on the far side of the ring, and in the blink of an eye, Min threw her feet through the middle and top ropes, hitting the Original Pulp Hero in the face with both feet while holding onto the top rope. He staggered backwards, as the Cantonese Kick Machine pulled herself back in over the top rope, just before Sheffield lunged forward to grab her. She then slingshot herself out with a corkscrew plancha, directly hitting and knocking the ACW's Heart & Soul down onto the black protective mats. Min stumbled to her feet, fuckin’ set to put what K-Wolf did to her on COURAGE 130 to SHAME, then the she whipped the Negasonic Lupine’s Vox Nihili-mate hard into the railing back-first before chopping him hard across the chest, getting a massive "Whoo!" reaction from the audience. Yeah, you better believe Flair was still alive in Roma! Li Mu Bai was telling Ms. Min to get back into the ring, and it seemed like she was going to listen, but instead climbed onto the turnbuckle, jumped onto the middle rope, and then hit Sheffield with aN asai moonsault! Shit was that angry Goddess Of FIGHT pulling out all the stops to kick ol’ Sheffield down as many pegs as possible. Min, however, landed on the other side of the railing, and fell into the lap of an older Italian woman, who quickly pushed her off. Oh, how Fejona Min wanted to blast her one in her looooud Italian mouth, but instead climbed onto the railing, then grabbed Sheffield’s head and bulldogged him to the protective mats. Fejona Min stood up and let the boos rain down upon her. Spot #30 in the End Game Match… here she comes. Sheffield was lifted up and rolled back into the ring by Fejona Min, who took her time in climbing onto the apron, and then heading to the top turnbuckle. All that time she took allowed Sheffield to jump to his feet, race into the corner, climb them, and toss Fejona from the turnbuckles with an old-fashioned Big Fat Kill! Fejona Min went flying half-way across the ring on his top rope belly-to-belly suplex, which now shared a name with his new finisher, but Sheffield could not capitalize on his move, thanks to the brutal beating he had just suffered at the hands of his opponent. "SHEFF-I-ELD! SHEFF-I-ELD! SHEFF-I-ELD!” The chant rang out throughout Stadio Olimpico, with everyone stomping their feet and clapping their hands in support of the Original Pulp Hero, and let’s not forget this wasn’t Sheffield’s first Beginning Of The End Match either; he had fought in and WON the very first. Li Mu Bai started his count, but once he reached nine, both competitors were holding onto the ropes and up on their feet. The two of them advanced towards each other, but it was Fejona Min who attacked first, dropkicking Sheff's knees out from under him. BACK FIST! BLOCKED! Luckily, Chris Sheffield had the quick reflexes to block the spinning back fist, otherwise it was going to be him in the cat bird seat of the End Game Match, that #1 spot. Speaking of that, you wouldn’t believe who was had been spotted backstage, well… he was still backstage, sitting in front of a monitor, now drinking a bottle of water. Ahem, that’s all I’ll say. At any rate, Sheff punched Fejona Min right in the ovaries while still holding onto her arm, and whipped her into the ropes. Tilt-a-whirl... shoulderbreaker? Fucking right it was. Sheffield took full advantage, putting his foot into Min's back and pulling back hard on the arms. Fejona screamed out in pain and let a few expletives fly such as "fuck", "goddamn", and my personal favorite: "son of a Sheffield". Did it make sense? Hell no, but was funny nonetheless. To say that the Killer Queen wasn't happy would have been a colossal understatement. But Sheff dropped her, as Min wasn't going to give up, and immediately followed up with a hard elbow into her back. Sheffield rolled Min over and covered her with a hook of the leg. "One!" "Two!" "She's up." Sheff didn't argue the count with Li, deciding to just shrugged his shoulders. The Man Without An Alias, instead, picking up Min and whipped her into the corner. Fejona hit with her side and busted right back out, only to run into a hiptoss, again landing on her side. When Fejona pushed herself up, Sheff quickly put her into the abdominal stretch. He pounded on Min’s ribs and stomach for a bit before the Cabalite started to get free. She pulled her arm free from behind Sheff's leg and reached up and grabbed the Pulp Hero's head, pulling him over into a small package! One! Two! Kick-out! "Ruuuaaaah!" was the unanimous reaction of the Roman crowd. Fejona was on her knees when Sheffield grabbed her by the hair, but Min just grabbed Sheff’s belt and pulled him forward while falling backwards, sending the Pulp Hero into the ropes, causing him to hit his throat on the top rope. Yes, he was gagging for air, but Fejona didn't care as she utilized the school girl roll-up. One! Two! Thr-- Kick Out! Despite the trouble breathing, Sheff still had the wherewithal to kick out. Both combatants got up and Fejona kicked Sheff in the bread basket to assist in his inability to breath properly. Min grabbed Sheff's head and bent him down before starting to kick the Pulp Hero in the face with the top of her boots. Min was quite vicious with the quick-hitting kicks. After several of those, Min shot Sheff into the corner but missed his charge. She was ready for a counter attack, however, but the clothesline failed the Cambodian Cabalite as Sheffield ducked underneath it and quickly locked the Fejona Min into a full-nelson. Dragon Suplex! One. Two. Kick out. They got up. Sheffield blocked a right hand, and returned one of his own. Fejona stopped him from building off of his momentum again with a kick to the gut, and grabbed his wrist, tossing him into the opposite corner with an Irish whip. The Female Buzzsaw turned. She ran and leapt, crashing all her weight into the Man Without An Alias, who just happened to be playing possum! Sheffield clasped his hands around Min, hoisting her into what looked like a tight bear hug, and took a couple of steps from the corner. Flashing devilish grin, he let Min’s feet touch the mat for half a second, and tossed her into the corner with an overhead belly-to-belly suplex! The crowd went ballistic, having just watched that bitch Min land on the top of her head after bouncing from the turnbuckle padding, while turned upside down! Fejona was laid out in a heap in the corner, upper body twisted in the opposite direction of her legs, and Sheffield was loving that he had a much lighter opponent for a change even if she had fucked him up something fierce in this match so far he was set on returning the favor. Sheffield huffed, and quickly picked Fejona up off the mat, making the cutting motion across his neck as hooked him up for a suplex and sat her on the top rope as the crowd murmured into a roar. He climbed up in preparation for a superplex, but was shoved off. He quickly got to his feet. He took a few steps back, knowing full well that he was going to gain some momentum. Why, exactly? Because he was planning to sprint up the ropes and deliver a SECOND good ol’ fashioned Big Fat Kill. It was that sort of night. “OOOH!” responded the Roman fans as they watched the Original Pulp Hero do just that, well… almost. He somehow mustered the agility and speed to get to the top, and once up there, grabbed a hold of Fejona in preparation for her execution LIVE on PPV. He steadied himself, until that bitch Fejona Min, eyes blazing in that ‘dream on, Sheffield’ sort of way, shoved him backwards off the top rope. This time he landed HARD with the back of his head smacking against the mat because he hadn’t tucked his chin properly. BOOM! He crashed hard on the mat, body mangled and twisted in every which way, while Fejona stood perched on the top rope (still dazed from, you know, getting dropped on her head earlier), allowing her to dive off, tuck once, and connect with a devastating frog splash. All the air left Sheff’s body. He curled up on the mat, gasping and wheezing in an attempt to feed air to empty lungs. That Fejona sure knew how to take his breath away, god DAMN. “Fuuuuck,” groaned Chris, face screwed up uncomfortably. He rolled onto his side, swatting Fejona’s hands away as she tried to make the cover. Fejona stood up, only to drop a knee across the forehead of Chris Sheffield. He sprung to his feet, obviously hurting as he’d taken a few seconds to nurse his back, and continuing with his attack, catapulted off the middle rope and into a moonsault on top of the Pulp Hero. ONE! TWO! THR—NEGATRON~! You better believe the ACW's Heart & Soul got his shoulder up! But she didn't waste too much time and climbed back up the turnbuckles. Fancy Seeing You Here The Beginning Of The End had just given an already excited crowd a small taste of what was surely to come in the hours to come and turned them rabid. Their combined noise, a spirited concoction of screaming, stomping, clapping, and whistling, shook the foundation of the Stadio Olimpico, a testament to the outstanding quality of All-Star Championship Wrestling’s content as of late -- a feat in itself considering that there was a battle for its control raging all the while. They had stormed Europe in a way that no one, from Napoleon to Hitler, ever could have and tonight their tour of Italy would culminate in Rome being brought to its knees. Separated from the massive audience by only a sheer, black fabric curtain, the hustling and bustling ACW crew members were working feverishly to keep the show running smoothly in the area that the World Wrestling Federation, before its days spent Entertaining, coined as the 'Gorilla Position'. They scurried like rats through the small, makeshift work station and they all seemed to be talking into headsets, though most likely not to each other. Chaos such as this was commonplace for Oso 'Bear' Bannon and so his demeanor, which looked calm and focused for a change, was unwavering when he entered it. Chester B. Wallace walked directly behind him, nodding at random crew members who hurried by in recognition, not for their contributions, but just because they were there. He watched as Oso snuck a peak out of the curtain and managed to catch a glimpse of the pandemonium outside it for himself. He smiled for it was he that had brought them there. Oso stretched his arms and legs, his mind going over countless strategies and plans of attack for the match that was vastly approaching. He had found his muse a few weeks back in Tyson XL and since then he had been spending his days training relentlessly and his nights sleeping easier than he had in over a year. He had struggled since his arrival to fit into ACW’s puzzle and knew that only a victory against The Night Life’s enforcer could help him craft the missing piece. He remembered the smug way XL had spoken to him the very first time they met and rubbed the back of his head to feel the almost healed bump the man known as 'Heavy T' (and 'Heavy Hitter' by a select few) had given him just a short while later. He had wondered then what Tyson’s motivation may have been, but no longer cared. Actually, he was thankful, for it was the gift of those bumps that had Oso Bannon finally feeling like wrestler who was worthy and capable of walking out of END GAME 2010 with championship silverd. Suddenly the air in the hastily thrown together room began to reek of cigarettes and also the faint smell of rapidly decomposing morals. “Excuse me, uh... Mr. Amazing One, uh... sir?” The Amazing Gabriel had entered the scene and was raising his eyebrows at a particularly sheepish looking ACW staff member holding a clipboard who was standing in his way. “Well aren’t you just an adorable little rule-following worker bee?” Gabriel sneered, looking down at the little man who still didn’t budge even after the blatant display of sarcasm and depreciation. “Ugh! Fine!” He slammed his cigarette down on the man’s clipboard and shoved him out of the way, letting the still partially smoking butt fall to the ground. Only then could he finally move forward and reveal Tyson XL who came out from the shadows behind him, towering over his leader just as any great enforcer should. He locked eyes with Oso, who surprisingly didn’t avoid contact with him, but it only lasted briefly for Chester Wallace had started to whisper into the Bear’s ear. Heavy T stood there in silence with The Amazing Gabriel, watching. It may have been slightly uncharacteristic, but he felt a sliver of sympathy for Bannon. It was advice like the type he was undoubtedly receiving from Wallace now that had brought him here in the first place, urging brought on by a business deal with his quote-unquote Amazing partner that the [Black] Scorpion Champion had only just learned about on the last episode of COURAGE. And yes, it bothered him, but the plan was still to, as the infamous “they” say, “play ball”. And so, while Oso Bannon may have finally found his spark, his fire... Tyson XL’s only intention on this occasion was to extinguish it, no matter the reason or if it was prematurely. The Amazing Gabriel rubbed his shoulders. “You ready to go, big man?” XL nodded without making a sound, disinterest disguised as intensity. A siren began to play over the loud speakers and the small crack of light that came through that old, sheer curtain dissipated, signifying that the house lights had dimmed. That same crew member, burn hole on the top pages of his clipboard and all, approached Oso and Wallace and gave them the thumbs up. Wallace held the curtains open for his client and exclaimed as Bear walked through 'em. “This is it!” Flashbulbs fired at the former football star like bullets at Normandy, fruits of the agent’s often dirty work. Not yet. LEADERSHIP OF THE PACK MATCH - STANDARD RULES Maddox St. James vs. Arkady Rasputin REFEREE: LEON HURST UNDEAD!!! Maddox was focused on the forthcoming battle and not in the mood to play diplomat, so he shook his head. "So be it." Maddox was quick to pull up his fists into a traditional boxing stance where he bounced up and down on the balls of his feet. On the other hand, Arkady brought his fists up and stomped his leading left foot down firmly into the mat. Maddox looked to start the battle, but after he took just a step in Arkady's direction, the Wolf halted him by holding his hand up. Arkady carefully and slowly adjusted his knee pad and did a few squats to loosen up. Despite his anger, Maddox was an honorable fighter and allowed his opponent to get ready. Arkady took a deep breath and positioned himself across from Maddox, once again getting into his Ong Bak style Muay Thai stance. The Sub-Zero Soldier immediately returned to his boxing-like stance and tried to charge once again. However, Arkady still was not yet ready to jump into a fight. The crowd wasn't sure of what Arkady was doing with his stretching, while Maddox was starting to grow even more furious. It wasn't like the Russian Wolf to delay a fight with anyone. He was a wrecking machine and everyone knew it. Yet, there he was, taking every spare moment he could to casually stretch. After once again taking a deep breath, Arkady looked to his foe and motioned for Maddox to come out of the corner where he was relaxing against the turnbuckle. He was only there for a second, as he fell to his back to avoid a knee strike to the head with the same leg, and he nipped back up to his feet. Maddox instantly turned around and had to immediately use the double forearm block again to halt a right-footed sidekick. Arkady never lowedered his foot and tried to force his foot through the block with a second thrust, only to have Maddox bend forward to arc his body around the kick. Still without dropping his leg, Arkady swung it forcefully and barely missed catching Maddox in the face. Using the momentum of the miss, Arkady spun into a roundhouse with a the same leg and struck his foe right in the kisser. Maddox stumbled backwards, but was back to his fighting stance in a flash and just in time for Arkady to rush him once again. The Wolf leapt into the air for a knee with his left leg. Maddox deflected it to the outside, which allowed Arkady to twist quickly and drive his right knee into Maddox's chest. That one put the Silent Spartan down. Yet, he wouldn't stay there, as he rolled backwards to his feet and immediate dove head-first at his enemy, simultaneously knocking down a knee with his left hand and punching his foe in the gut with a right hand. Landing in a three-point stance, Maddox anchored his hand to the mat and swung his leg around for a sweep that Arkady stepped over. However, Maddox spun like a top and connected with a pitter patter like combination of kicks that struck Arkady's abdomen, forcing him to back up. Showing that he was not intimidated, Arkady slammed his foot down violently to once again take his stance. Maddox was feeling it now and went right back to work, not wanting to let his rival breathe. A initial right jab caught nothing but air, but the second had to be deflected by Arkady's left hand and was immediately followed by a left had that also needed to be brushed aside. Arkady instantly fired back with a over the top elbow, which Maddox was able to side step and counter with another pair of right hands that were stopped by Arkady's left arm. However, the Russian Wolf was forced to duck under a lighting fast left hand that followed the rights and twist to avoid a huge right uppercut that could've easily knocked his chin up through his nose. A wild left hook by the Silent Spartan was battled down by Arkady's left hand and the Wolf spun with the motion. A leaping back roundhouse missed, but just as he hit the mat, he made Maddox block a knee strike aimed for the deaf warrior's chest. With neither man gaining the upper hand, they each took a step back while never lowering their guard. "ACW! ACW! ACW!" The fight sequence brought the crowd to their feet, as they couldn't believe the speed of the two warriors, as all of the action happened in what seemed like the blink of an eye. Yet, things were only getting started for the two warriors. Arkady took a moment to examine Maddox's stance. Well, to be honest, he only had a moment. Maddox was not going to let his rival get a second's rest and went right back on the attack. A left-right-left combination by the Silent Spartan wielded no results and he had to quickly step back to avoid Arkady's left and right elbow in that order. The Wolf would not let up, but had his left-right combination of punches blocked. The people watching on could not believe the speed of Arkady's strikes or the speed of Maddox who bobbed and weaved like Neo out of the Matrix to avoid them, as lefts and rights of all shapes and sizes were fired at the FrankenTiger Mask. Finally, Maddox saw an opening and retaliated with a right Muay Thai elbow of his own that Arkady was barely able to block in time, as it looked to connect with the side of his head. Adapting on the fly, Maddox saw how close he was to his foe and changed things up by using more close range strikes. A left elbow was blocked and a second whiffed an attempt at Arkady's jaw. The same combination was attempted immediately and got the same result, but this time, Maddox struck with a knee to Arkady's ribs that sent him stumbling back and in perfect position for Maddox to strike him in the chest with a jumping knee strike to the chest. Before Arkady could react to being hit, Maddox locked his hands at the base of the Wolf's skull and pulled him into a knee to the ribs. The Silent Spartan was looking to one up his foe, but that knee was the only one that would find it's mark, as Arkady blocked three in a row with his forearms. With a roar, the Winter Soldier knocked Maddox's arms away, but found them instantly around his on his traps. A sledgehammer like strike with each hand forced Maddox's elbows to buckle and the Canadian was sent stumbling back as the fists struck him in the chest. "ACW! ACW! ACW!" The crowd once again showed their approval, while the two warriors glared at one another. "Is that stance something Xiong showed you," Arkady remarked, as he lowered his hands. "What style is it?" Maddox pointed to himself and chopped his right arm like he was rooting for the Florida State Seminoles. He pointed to his eye and then to infront of himself in one motion before making a fist with his pinky extended and quickly closing the hand fully, but with his thumb in between his middle and index finger. He touched the tips of his two index fingers and finally, pointed to Arkady. "Let's see if I can learn it..." Maddox couldn't believe his eyes, as Arkady raised his fists and started bouncing, mimicking his own stance. Even going as far as to switch quickly between righty to south paw a couple of times. The Wolf fired first with a right hand and a roundhouse from the same side. Both were blocked and Maddox tried for a kick of his own which too was blocked. Both men had the same idea and tried for a low roundhouse and ended up slamming their shins into one another. The Sub-Zero Soldier ignored the pain in his leg from it colliding with Arkady's and tried for a snap roundhouse to the face. It missed it's target and he had to lean back to avoid an identical kick from his rival. Both men tried for a spinning backfist and had their forearms crash together. Maddox tried for a right hand only to have it batted down and have to halt one himself and deflect a second punch aimed for his gut. The Silent Spartan hoped a right cross would turn the momentum in his favor, but it got nothing but air. The two men traded low kicks, each one successful in blocking the other, and Arkady launched an elbow at Maddox. Much like the Winter Soldier had to earlier, Maddox was able to curl his arm up beside his head to block the strike, but Arkady pushed down on it trying to force his way through. Maddox directed the force downward and tried for an elbow of his own, but it got nothing but his foe's forearm. Instead of trying to divert the force, Arkady struck his rival in the ribs with a knee. In a split-second, Arkady had Maddox in a clinch and started firing knee after knee, pinning him against the ropes. The Canadian was able to block them and grabbed his enemy by the belt of his trunks. The grip allowed Maddox to push the pair away from the ropes. He had hoped to pin Arkady against the ropes, but in the middle of the ring, the wold repositioned his feet, planting them firmly and stopping all momentum in a flash. The Frankentiger Mask brushed Arkady's arms away and looked for a double fisted punch to the chest only to have his arms caught at the wrist. A pair of headbutts connected with the Russian's chest and he barely ducked under a roundhouse kick followed by a wild right hand. The motion of the punch spun Maddox around so he had his back to Arkady and he tried for a back hardspring into a kick. The Winter Soldier caught Maddox by his ankles and pulled him into a handstand. A boot to the face was avoided by Maddox tilting to his left, balancing on that one hand alone. Never pulling his foot back, Arkady looked to sweep the arm out from under his rival, so Maddox executed a forward roll, as he pushed himself over the incoming kick. Getting to one knee, as he twisted mid-roll, the Sub-Zero Soldier escaped having his jaw broken by Arkady with a shining wizard. He captured the Wolf's left ankle, as the right leg sped over his head, knowing his rival would land safely on his feet. Maddox rose to a vertical base only to have Arkady try to clip his head with a reverse enziguiri. The Winter Soldier's balance was incredible, as he once again landed safely with only one foot. Knowing his opponent well, the Wolf knew what Maddox would do in this position. As the Canadian twisted to the mat clockwise, Arkady did the same, freeing his foot and saving him from the Mandala Hineri. Both men rolled to their feet and simultaneously threw jumping knee strikes, each one successful. The collision brought both men down to the mat, where they landed on one knee within inches of eachother. They swung with all they had, not even trying to stand up. Their fists slammed together like a pair of rams fighting over a mate and each one tried to make the other's arm buckle, but after just a few seconds, they knew it would never happen. "ACW! ACW! ACW!" The two men pulled back their respective fists, never once taking their eyes off each other, with the crowd cheering them on. "SI-LENT SPAR-TAN!!!" "RUS-SIAN WOLF!!!" "SI-LENT SPAR-TAN!!!" "RUS-SIAN WOLF!!!" The dueling chants thundered through the arena, as the crowd couldn't decide between the two and just wanted to see more. Both men knew that they weren't going to be able to overcome the other through striking, so it looked like things were going to turn to the realm of wrestling once more. As if on the same wavelength, they simultaneously tugged the top rop and began to circle. Your traditional collar-and-elbow tie-up followed and the two men began to jockey for position. Evenly matched in many ways, neither man could gain the upper hand. Arkady stomped his foot down on the mat firmly and turned up the power. Maddox was forced to take a step back, but he wasn't going to let himself be overpowered, so he did just the same and forced the pair back to where they started. The crowd could hear the two men growling at one another with neither able to assert their dominance over the other. Maddox looked to change things up by transtioning to a headlock, only to have Arkady do the same. Both men bent over with a strong side headlock clamped on, forcing their temples to be pressed together. Maddox was the first to apply more pressure, but Arkady wouldn't release his grip due to it and quickly returned the favor. Both men squeezed as hard as they could, causing the other to snarl upon doing so. However, neither were content with just standing there, so they did a forward roll, each hoping to slip out upon doing so. The holds just couldn't be broken and now, the two were sitting with the same hold applied. It was easy to see that both men were growing frustrated that they couldn't break the other. The broke their holds at the same time and rolled in opposite directions to all fours where they did a front handspring to their feet. Just as they were back at a vertical base, they fired a low roundhouse that made their shins slam together and immediately walloped eachother in the chest with huge kicks that made each man stumble backwards. Maddox and Arkady met in the middle of the ring, slamming their foreheads together while foaming of the mouth with rage. Neither would cower before the other and like a pair of rams, they battled while never once letting their foreheads part. Without warning, the two began unloading with forearms. These were not back and forth shots, but shots that connected at the same time. Again and again they struck one another with neither side showing any signs of stopping. About twenty strikes in, the crowd started to cheer and stomp their feet, pushing them to keep going. Twenty turned into twenty five... then thirty... then forty... then fifty... It looked like they would just keep going on forever until the two warriors took off to opposite sets of ropes. It was like an traffic collision in the center of the ring, as the two men met and delivered matching forearm shots. That rattled both men and they had trouble staying upright. However, a roar brought both men back up where they once again swapped blows. This time it was in the form of huge lariats that didn't budge either man. In the blink of an eye, they darted to the ropes and slid past each other as both tried for a baseball slide dropkick. Rising up to one knee mid-slide, the pair did forward rolls and Maddox looked to go for a lariat. Arkady knew the rolling Crystal Flash was one out of Maddox's sensai's playbook, as he has witnessed Aria use it before. Therefore, he was able to counter into a full nelson. Maddox was not going to be dumped on his head by one of the Wolf's trademark suplexes and slipped into a rear waistlock before Arkady could lock his fingers. The Winter Soldier slapped his foe's hands to break free, making sure to capture one wrist, so he could turn it into a top wristlock. The wristlock led to an Irish whip attempt, which was reversed by the Sub-Zero Soldier. Maddox leapt up for a dropkick and Arkady leapt up to meet him, looking to counter with his trademark twisting knee called "The Creeper". However, Maddox was counting on it. He snagged Akrady's knee in mid air, causing Arkady to land on his back, as Maddox landed on all fours on above Arkady, kinda like sixty nine position. In a flash, Maddox was locking in a spinning toehold. One rotation was not enough for the Canadian and he looked for a second. The Wolf was expecting it and rolled just as Maddox tried to apply the pressure, taking him down face first with a modified drop toehold. A chicken wing was locked on and as Arkady went for the crossface, hoping to hook the Mask of Voorhees, Maddox rolled the pair over and landed in a seat position next to the face up Russian. The Anaconda Vice was the Canadian's goal, but no luck. Arkady was able to slip his head past his own arm to avoid the hold and pull Maddox's arm into a head and arm capture, looking for a Koji clutch. However, when Arkady looked to position his leg, Maddox's free arm was able to push it down, forcing the Winter Soldier to roll over to his stomach. A quick crossing of his foe's leg and a slight rotation put the Silent Spartan in perfect position for the Haas of Pain. Arkady pushed his upper body off the mat, as soon as he saw the positioning and used the momentum of his foe attempting to apply the hold, to do a forward roll up to his feet. The Russan Wolf was off to the ropes in a flash and tried for his baseball slide knee with Maddox on one knee. It got nothing but air, as Maddox fell to his back. The two men lay on their backs with their scalps just an inch or two from touching. They rolled backwards, lacing their legs upon doing headstands. In another test of strength, the two men began lifting one hand from the mat just to strike the other with it. They couldn't care less about being upside down or having all their weight on their necks, as they went back and forth, socking each other in the jaw. Nine or ten shots later, the pair untied their legs and skillfully rolled to their feet where they looked to one another in their fighting stances, much to the delight of the capacity crowd. "ACW! ACW! ACW!" Maddox adjusted his kickpad and Arkady his elbowpad, as they began to circle once more. The two once again met in the center of the ring. This time it was Arkady crouching down with his hand raised, offering to lace fingers for a test of strength. Maddox collected his thoughts and wiped his hands off on his tights before mimicking his rival's actions. With their one side locked in battle, the Silent Spartan extended his left hand and it was quickly met by the Russian Wolf's right hand. Their chests slammed together. Very few matched up as evenly as the two members of the Pack and they just couldn't break their stalemate. It was growing more apparent that the usually rage-filled Arkady was keeping his cool, as the ever-cocky Maddox was becoming more and more enraged. Proof of this was the Sub-Zero Soldier, as forcefully knocking heads with his rival while they locked horns. Not going to back down an inch against any foe, especially someone he saw as a true rival in Maddox, Arkady gave him a headbutt right back. That led to a war of attrition between the two with Arkady gaining the upperhand with him landing three in a row. The flurry of headbutts allowed the Wolf to impose his will, forcing Maddox backwards. The Canadian would not break and bridged backwards. A true student of the game, Arkady had seen this before and positioned his right boot between Maddox's head and arm, leading to to him repatedly scraping his boot down along the side of his rival's face. However, Maddox would not break. Though, he did have another idea. The Sub-Zero Soldier waited for Arkady to scrape his face once again and in a flash, he grabbed the Winter Soldier by calf and twisted towards his foe while pulling the leg out from under him, causing Arkady to fall face-first to the mat. A quick placement of his legs and the Russian found himself nearly hanging upside down due to a standing gorilla clutch. Knowing that if he tried to head for the ropes, Maddox would scissor his body, so Arkady pushed up off the mat and violent twisted his body. He knew Maddox wouldn't break the hold, so it allowed him to wind up kneeling on Maddox's chest, where he started elbowing away at Frankentiger Mask's ribs. Unable to stop the elbows, no matter how much pressure he applied to the submission, Maddox shoved his rival forward and spin-a-roonied up to his feet while Arkady rolled to his feet. The Winter Soldier hit the ropes and and had to duck a spinning backfist. A spinning heel kick came flying at Maddox right after and he slipped under it while heading to the ropes Arkady just came from. Instead of hitting the mat hard after the miss, Arkady used the rotation to land on all fours. A front handspring later and the Russian Wolf tried hoped to catch the on-coming Maddox with a double knee strike. The Canadian used a double leg takedown to counter and put his rival on his back on the mat. It was no secret what Maddox was going for, as he locked one leg behind the other. It was the Canadian Cloverleaf. Arkady was counting on this and pulled Maddox's arm to him, as he slipped it between the Wolf's legs, looking to apply the submission. This sudden tug caught Maddox off-guard and he fell forward, his chin falling across Arkady's shin. Before the Canadian could react to what was happening, Arkady had locked on Hell's Gates. No one had expected such a move from Arkady. He had never used that move before and no one knew that it was even in his repitoire. Maddox struggled with all of his might, but the awkward submission was expertly applied and he had no idea how to escape it. All of his studying and not once did he plan for this. He knew that even the tiniest amount of time in this hold was enough to get a submission from most people and his time was growing larger and larger. Luckily, his counter to the double knee strike had positioned them close to the ropes. It took all of the Silent Spartan's might to drag Arkady just a few inches, so he could reach the bottom rope with his foot. Never one to abuse the count, the Russian Wolf released the hold and slipped to the opposite side of the ring. Maddox rubbed the back of his neck, as he stayed close to the ropes, watching his foe slowly rise up to a vertical base. There was no reason to hurry and waste energy, so Arkady took full advantage of the pause in the action. The Canadian did just the same before nodding at his rival, as if to say good work on the counter. Both had regained their composure and focus in no time and began to circle once more. It looked as if a second collar-and-elbow tie-up was in the works, but Maddox struck with a boot to the gut and a pair of stiff right hands. Not many throw punches better than the Frankentiger Mask and even Arkady had trouble keeping pace with them. A quick wrenching of the arm and Maddox applied a top wristlock. Arkady dropped to one knee and found his rival applying more pressure. He didn't remain there for long and just as he stood up, he turned the tables on his foe by applying one of his own. Maddox wasted no time in doing the well-known front flip then nip-up into a reversal. Arkady looked to do the same by rolling foward and curling up his legs. However, instead of launching himself off the mat, he kicked Maddox in the side of the head. The shot was more of a jab, but it allowed Arkady to roll with the kick and take the dazed Maddox down with a single leg takedown. He easily passed over the Sub-Zero Soldier's legs and locked on a side headlock, making sure to scissor Maddox's near arm. Gripping with all of his might, Arkady's plan to wear down his opponent was in full effect. Yet, it was short-lived. Maddox rolled the pair over, pinning his opponent's shoulders to the mat. 1... 2... There was really no chance of a three-count and Maddox knew that. However, it did make a great distraction that allowed him to lock Arkady in a headscissors upon retaking his seat position. Unlike most people, Arkady didn't rotate his body so he was at Maddox's feet. Instead, he rotated to the side of the Canadian's torso. As if he was working out, the Winter Soldier hopped from one side of Maddox to the other, making him think about it before executing a front handspring to his feet. Arkady hit the ropes in no time flat and he hopped over a face down Maddox. He ducked under a leapfrog and and stopped in his tracks, so he could smack Maddox in the chest with a roundhouse that made the arena moan. The Russian Wolf took off to the ropes only to have his opponent do the same and just when he turned to face him, Maddox bounced off the middle rope and tried for a tornado DDT. Arkady wouldn't be taken over. He placed Maddox on his feet and twisted out to hook a hammerlock. The hammerlock was only on for a moment, when Arkady hooked a chickenwing instead and clamped on a crossface. The Canadian saw his life flash before his eyes and he hurried backwards with all of his might, slamming Arkady into the corner. Impact with the corner was all Maddox needed and he was able to snapmare his foe over so he could rattle his spine with a huge soccer kick to the back. The crowd reacted to that kick just as they did with Arkady's previous one and the Frankentiger Mask darted to the ropes. A sliding clothesline was caught and Arkady was able to use the momentum to roll backwards to his feet while Maddox went face-down on the mat. Never once releasing his enemy's arm, the Winter Soldier struck his rival square in the top of the head with a knee and quickly went for a la mahistral cradle. Before the referee could even get into position to count the fall, Maddox kicked out. In doing so, he landed on his feet and caught his foe in the mouth with a low dropkick. With his jaw rocked, Arkady rolled to the outside. The Sub-Zero Soldier saw this as his chance to gain the upper hand for sure and he was going to take it. Using the far ropes to build up a head of steam, Maddox looked to the skies and leapt all the way up to the top rope with no hands. Much to his surprise, Arkady was on the apron waiting for him. The Russian Wolf knew the positioning well and exactly what his opponent would look to do. Going with his gut, he leapt up to the apron just as Maddox leapt up to the top rope. Once there, he swatted at his opponent's legs only to have Maddox leap over his arm and land in the ring. Maddox showed his quickness by avoiding a kick and faked a shoulder between the ropes, baiting Arkady into throwing a knee. The Wolf fell for it, hook, line, and sinker. The Sub-Zero Soldier was able to snag his foe's leg and torque the leg harshly with a dragon screw, as the ropes kept Arkady vertical. This was an opportune time to strike as any and Maddox was fully aware of it. He bounced off the adjacent middle rope and catching his rival with a Kawada kick right to the mouth. Arkady tumbled to the floor and wasn't sure what to attend to first, his leg or his mouth. That was enough of a distraction for Maddox to go airborn. 720 SPACEMAN PLANCHA!!! No one could believe the height that Maddox got on that corkscrew attack and Maddox burst back up to his feet to bask in the roar of the crowd. Once getting his fill, the Sub-Zero Soldier broke the count and focused his attention back on his lupine rival. With the clock restarted on their time outside of the ring, Maddox struck the Russian Wolf's thigh with a pair of kicks and whipped him into the barricade where he immediately followed with with a low dropkick that sandwiched Arkady's right leg the barricade and the sole of Maddox's boots. That caused the Winter Soldier to crumble to the floor and his leg was quickly held up against the barrier, so it could be struck repeatedly with kicks, Maddox continuing to use the barricade as an ally. Wanting to make it clear that he should be the alpha, Maddox looked only to pin his foe or make him tap out. He knew that he couldn't do that on the outside, so he fired Akady back into the ring. Maddox waited patiently for Arkady to rise back up, making him use his leg, before unloading with a flurry in the form of a trio of kicks to the right thigh, a left hook, and martial arts style spinning legsweep. A quick hop over his foe and Maddox ricochetted off the middle with a double knee right to Arkady's damaged leg. The Silent Spartan was well-known for his targetting of a foe's leg and he would not disappoint. Positioning his feet on each side of Arkady's hurting leg, Maddox picked up his ankle and a small rotation led to a figure four leglock. Snarling the entire time, Arkady tried to block out the pain that coursed through his damaged leg and he pushed his back up off the mat with Maddox doing the same, trying to apply more pressure. "This all you got for me?" the Russian Wolf growled at his rival, not showing any fear or pain. "You don't want it bad enough." That got a reaction out of the Canadian. SMACK! "You could never be the alpha," Arkady informed him after receiving a loud open hand shot to the face. Maddox wound up for another, but that was exactly what his rival hoped he would do. Arkady caught the incoming hand and pulled it, while rotating with the momentum and rolling the two fighters over. The rotation was all the Winter Soldier needed to reach the bottom rope and get the break. Maddox pounced right on his foe, stomping at his knee before pulling him up. Showing some strength of his own, Maddox lifted Arkady up onto his shoulders for a torture rack. He wrapped his arm around his foe's injured leg and tossed his upper body into the air. The Sub-Zero Soldier fell to one knee hitting a high impact version of a knee breaker. Keeping a firm lock on Arkady's knee, Maddox made sure Arkady fell face first to the mat. This was so he could quickly lock on an Indian Deathlock. With a boom, the Canadian fighter fell to his back. However, like these two are known to do, once is never enough. BOOM! BOOM! Just as soon as he got back to his feet, Maddox rotated one hundred and eighty degree with Arkady's legs intwined with his. A slap to his sides forced the Wolf's arms back and right into maddox's grasp. CURBSTOMP! That one nearly broke Arkady's nose and Maddox looked down at his hurt rival. He raised his hand victoriously, saying without words that he should be the alpha, which got a mixed reaction from the crowd. Once finished, he returned his focus to his foe, as Arkady battled to stand back up. Not once forgetting about his foe's injured body part, the Silent Spartan kicked away at Arkady's leg before stepping up onto his knee. CRIMSON DYNAMO! Well, that was the idea. However, Arkady wasn't going to let his jaw be torn from his face, so he ducked under it. Maddox landed on all fours, as his opponent took off to the ropes. A low dropkick took the Russian Wolf's right leg out from under him, forcing him to do a forward roll to avoid having his face slamming into the mat. The safety maneuver put Arkady at the ropes and his rival came right at him. Tiger Feint Zero looked to be on target, but once again, the Winter Soldier knew his foe all too well. As Maddox spun over the top rope, Arkady was able to catch him in a fireman's carry. GO2SLEEP! Holding a half nelson with his right arm to keep Maddox from falling, the Wolf immediately took him vertical. FISHERMAN'S BUSTER! Rolling the pair back up almost instantaneously, kept that half nelson intact and shot his left arm between his opponent's legs from behind while twisting counter-clockwise to the mat, slamming Maddox back on his head and neck. SPRINGWOOD SLASHER!!! Maddox had not seen Arkady use that move in years and he was just dumped on his head by it. With their knowledge of each other being so great, Arkady was bringing out some tatics that ACW and more importantly Maddox had not seen from him before. Arkady would never admit that he was hurting in any way, shape, or form, but using the Go2Sleep with his injured leg was not exactly the brightest idea. Though, it did do the trick. It took a moment to massage out some of the pain that had engulfed his leg, but he did finally rise up to his feet. He tried shaking out the soreness in his leg upon standing, while his rival was seeing tweety birds revolving his own cranium. A final stretch of his leg and he met Maddox, as the Sub-Zero Soldier got to his knees. SMACK!!! When you step into the ring with Arkady, you know you will have his brand of kickpad etched in your chest, whether you like it or not. SMACK!!! Another one found its mark. SMACK!!! Maddox fell forward with that one, instinctively using his arms to keep him from falling to the mat. Arkady didn't care if his foe hit the mat or not, as he grabbed Maddox by the back of the head and flat out started kicking the shit out him. Short, left-footed Kawada kicks blasted the Canadian again and again in the face. Arkady showed no signs of stopping, even with his foe becoming lifeless and only being held up by the Wolf's tenacious grip. The crowd cheered him on, wanting to see how many kicks he could unleash before becoming tired. At the forty mark, Arkady gave a giant tug of Maddox's hair, forcing him up just long enough for... SMACK!!! A final kick rocked Maddox's head, knocking him to his back. The repeated use of his leg took it's toll on Arkady and he fell to one knee while the crowd cheered in approval. He hoped this slight rest would help remove the pressure on his knees that came from standing. Once again grabbing his foe by the hair, Arkady pulled Maddox up and sent him stumbling back into the corner with a huge European uppercut. An Irish whip sent Maddox racing towards the opposite corner where he easily leapt all the way up to the top rope and took to the skies, diving over the incoming Arkady into a forward roll. The Wolf exploded out of the corner and Maddox took his foe up for his tilt-a-whirl powerbomb. Arkady slipped free, landed on all fours at his foe's side, and rose up right into a rear waistlock on Maddox. A German suplex spiked the Canadian on his head. The Russian Wolf was not satisfied with just one. His grip was tight and he pulled Maddox back up. The Sub-Zero Soldier drove his heel into Arkady's right knee, allowing him to break loose and return the favor with a German suplex of his own. Just like his rival had done earlier, Maddox held on and planted him with a second German suplex. Never once losing his grip, Maddox looked to transition to a half nelson suplex. Arkady was having none of it and elbowed Maddox with his captured arm. Before the Canadian could react to being hit, Arkady had spun and started to lift him for the now well-known FUKSZ. Landing behind Arkady courtesy of a float over, Maddox looked to turn the tide with a second German suplex. The Winter Soldier tried to once again use his lethal elbows to escape, but the Sub-Zero Soldier ducked and took Arkady over with a northern lights suplex. Arkady was able to lock his foe in a front chancery, as he was lifted, and in doing so, Maddox had to do roll with his suplex or have his ripped from his shoudlers. This allowed Arkady to roll the pair over so he was on top with his feet planted on the mat and instead of scisssoring his foe's body for a front choke, Arkady pulled Maddox up and drove him headfirst into the mat. FUKSZ!!! A rare cover... 1... 2... NO! That very well could have spelled defeat for the Frankentiger Mask, but lady luck was on his side. He landed close to the ropes and while his brain was scrambled, it knew enough to put his foot on the ropes. As soon as Arkady rose off of him, Maddox rolled out to the apron. However, that didn't save him from his rival who pulled him up to a vertical base by the hair as soon as Arkady finished tending to his wounded knee. The delay did allow Maddox to recover enough energy to try again to fake his foe into making a mistake. He faked a shoulder block between the ropes and like before, Arkady went to counter it with a knee. This time, when Maddox reached in to grab his leg, the Winter Soldier pulled the Canadian's torso through the ropes and... SMACK!!! SMACK!!! SMACK!!! SMACK!!! A quartet of punts to the face sounded like a machine gun firing and Arkady sent his rival crashing to the floor with a one-handed push to the top of the head. Arkady did a couple of squats and shook out his leg while waiting for Maddox to show signs of life on the outside. Seeing his foe starting to stir, Arkady hit the far ropes and displayed some of his rarely seen agility. BLIND SPACEMAN MOONSAULT!!! ACW! ACW! ACW! For the first time since joining ACW, Arkady broke out his big time high risk move. Despite the crowd chanting, Arkady's adrenaline couldn't overcome the fact that his leg was not ready that and he could barely put any weight on it. Though, that didn't stop him from slamming Maddox's face into the security barrier and rocking him with a set of six vicious Muay Thai elbows to the side of his head. Arkady rolled the woozy Canadian into the ring and slid in after him. He knew his leg couldn't handle much more activity like that, so he decided to change things up with a tazmission. Perhaps he should have gone for a cover, but Arkady wanted to make sure he kept the pressure on. Maddox was having difficulty focusing and things were starting to get dark. He knew he had to do something and do it fast. What energy he had left was directed to getting back to his feet. His mind raced with every counter he could think of and he chose the most basic. He backed the pair into the corner, crushing Arkady between himself and the turnbuckle. "F*ck you," Arkady snarled, never breaking the hold. "Try harder." So, Maddox did just that, backing his foe into the corner once again with exponentially more power and then, sat out with a jawbreaker. However, the Russian Wolf just wouldn't let get go. "That all you got?" Arkady roared. The Silent Spartan looked to do it a third time and found that Arkady would not allow him, positioning the duo so Arkady's back never faced the corner. Adapting to the situation, Maddox ran towards the corner and up it, pushing off the top. This forced his rival to his back with him on top, where he rolled back to his feet. Instead of going for a pin while being in the submission, Maddox slipped free. He instantly sat his foe up and with a hand full of hair delivered a huge soccer kick to the back. Unfortunately for Arkady, It wasn't just a single a kick. SMACK!!! SMACK!!! SMACK!!! SMACK!!! SMACK!!! Maddox gave his rival a taste of his own medicine and violently slammed his head down so he could drive all the wind out of Arkady with a brutal double stomp to the chest before ricochetting off the middle ropes right to the top like he was going for a BME. However, he drove both his feet down into Arkady's chest with a twisting double stomp. That burst had finished off what energy the Canadian had left eventhough it left the Wolf lifeless on the mat. The referee had no choice but to start the standing ten-count. 1... 2... 3... 4... 5... 6... 7... 8... 9... Both men had to make use of the ropes to make it back to their feet in time and was definitely just in the nick of time. Arkady dragged his leg behind him, as he joined Maddox in the center of the ring. With his main offensive weapon in need of repairs, the Wolf tried to get into a boxing match with his foe. Normally, that would be a fine idea, as very few can keep pace with him in the striking department. However, the Silent Spartan was one of those few. They took turns unloading with right hands, until Maddox bobbed under one. This small opening allowed Maddox to do what he does best. A pair of rights to the jaw were followed by a left. They were followed by a right-left combination to the gut and a right uppercut that took Arkady off his feet. The flurry lit a fire in the Sub-Zero Soldier and he dashed off to the far ropes. JUMPING CALF KICK! In the blink of an eye, Maddox was back up and off the ropes once again. ROLLING CRYSTAL FLASH! The rolling thunder clothesline nearly flipped the Wolf completely backwards and maybe he wished it did, as he landed on his head and neck harshly. He had no time to tend to his wounds, as Maddox picked him up and in no time, had him up for a powerbomb. The Canadian carried him quickly over to corner where he slammed him spine first into the top turnbuckle. Arkady staggered out of the corner and right into an on-coming freight train. SICK KICK! A cover followed... 1... 2... Thr-NO!!! Arkady had to dig deep and use his upper body to excape defeat. Maddox wasn't sure about that count, as he was positive it was three, and he glared at the referee. The Sub-Zero Soldier made guns with his hands and pressed them to his temples, forecasting what was to come. A quick crossing of Arkady's arms and Maddox twisted him around so the straightjacket was across the Wolf's throat. Refusing to say die, the Russian Wolf would not stay down and the crowd couldn't believe it. Maddox took a deep breath and looked down at the mat in disbelief that he couldn't put away his teammate. He slapped the mat in rage and hurried to the furthest corner. He began huffing and puffing, building up the adrenaline that was now coursing through his veins. DRAGON SUPLEX!!! Holding on... TAZMISSION-PLEX!!! Still not done... COBRA CLUTCH SUPLEX!!! And that's not all! Dead center of the ring, Maddox had repeatedly dumped Arkady on his head, he took a page out of his rival's playbook with the suplex chain, and now, had one of both men's trademark holds applied. He cranked back with all he had, wanting to do what no one else had done in ACW. He wanted to defeat Arkady and take his place as the alpha male of the pack. Roaring, as he pulled back on his foe, Maddox looked to have this one firmly in his grasp. There was no chance of Arkady reaching the ropes or having someone save him. However, he refused to tap. Each reply was more forcefully than the last and soon Arkady was roaring back at Maddox. If the Silent Spartan could talk, he still would have been speechless over what happened next. No one could ever have predicted that Arkady would have pulled his arm out from behind his head and had it between the two warriors. RED MIST!!! That attack came out of left field and had turned the tide in the blink of an eye. Arkady quickly turned the tables on his foe, forcing his opponent's arm into anaconda vice position. With his right hand, holding Maddox's arm in place for the vice, Arkady declined going for that submission. Instead, he slipped his left arm between the bicep of Maddox's trapped arm and head, placing his forearm against one of the large arteries in Maddox's neck. His left hand gripped his right arm and Arkady's modified choke was locked in. The Silent Spartan struggled to break free, but no matter which way he pushed, the hold seemed to get worse. If he pulled back, his head pushed against his arm, pushing Arkady's forearm tighter against his neck. If he pushed forward, Arkady pushed his forearm harder against his neck. No matter what, Maddox would not give in, but he had no way to escape. He refused to tap and held his arm high in the air. "PLEASE DON'T TAP! PLEASE DON'T TAP! PLEASE DON'T TAP!" With each moment that passed, his arm came closer and closer to the mat. Soon, his hand was on the mat with his eyes shut and the referee hurried to check on him. Once... Twice... Three times and it's over. The referee quickly called for the bell, knowing Maddox couldn't stay in the hold any longer. The Russian released the hold and fell to his back next to his unconscious rival. Arkady was exhausted and breathed heavily, as he tried to recover enough just to move. The war had come to an end and there was only one undisputed alpha of the pack. His name was Arkady Rasputin. WINNER: Arkady via SUBMISSION. ACW's Hall Of Fame Induction - 'The One' Jimmy Reid The spotlight fell on the podium on the stage. It was time. It was time, for people to stand up and get hyped about the next induction into ACW's Hall Of Fame. Although, when the cameras panned over to the Broadcast Booth, the fans at home instantly knew not to get all that excited. The look of discontent on Renaud Cardinal's face spoke volumes. RENAUD: Ladies and gentlemen, it's time for another person to be inducted into the Hall Of Fame. Initially, a lot of people were excited about this induction. I was supremely thrilled, because myself and another individual were selected to induct this... individual into the Hall Of Fame.
