But first, let's take a gander at these questions. Why did you punch Renaud Cardinal in the face? Why did you reveal yourself to be in cahoots with Z? ONE MAN'S END - STANDARD RULES Ryan Billows vs. Midnight Cowboy REFEREE: LI MU BAI 15 July 2010, one particular Lion had played phoenix, rising up from the ashes of a career the entire world had considered dead and done. A guy breaks his back, you'd think that'd be it. Game over. But, apparently, the game wasn't over. It was just beginning. Three years on the shelf, and Billows had returned and he had gone about his mission, his vengeful, bitter, little mission of doing all things possible to get underneath his once adoring fans' skins. And, in Billows' mind, it had been nothing more than going after one of the crowd's most loved: Midnight Cowboy. It had seemed simple enough, but since the whole thing had begun, it had morphed into something personal. Both men had been pushed to their limit. Billows had been growing exhausted with every fiber that made up Midnight Cowboy, and when Midnight Cowboy recruited his old friend, Jonny Yankee, out of retirement—Billows had been pushed over the edge. And Midnight Cowboy? He'd long been at his wit's end with The Risen Lion. IN ACROPOLIS had seen Midnight Cowboy send Billows careening off of a semi-truck's trailer (granted, it was a little payback for Billows sending Cowboy off a cliff through a hotdog stand...). The following COURAGE had seen Billows fake a re-injured back, giving an oscar-worthy performance of paralysis. When The Smooth Operator had fallen into the dupe, somehow managing to feel regret for his actions, he'd nearly found himself with a smashed head at the end of it...that was the night that The Smooth Operator was driven past his wit's end... The friends brought out of retirement. The constant who-could-drive-who-crazy. The breaking of a particular horse's leg. It all led up to this. Billows had finally folded on the most recent COURAGE, and he'd agreed to Midnight Cowboy's call for a Loser Leaves ACW challenge. Tonight, at END GAME 2010, for Billows and Cowboy.... it was ONE MAN'S END. And here's Tommy Vale on the microphone, “The following match will be fought under the following stipulations: One man MUST be declared the winner. The other man...the other man will bid farewell to ACW. There will be no draw. Introducing first...” And Tommy Vale went about introducing each of our competitors for this huge match, the crowd was already stirring, anticipating the arrival of one of their most loved, Midnight Cowboy, or would it possibly that, coming out first tonight... The lights fell to black, leaving the crowd in a canopy of darkness, the camera flashes resembling stars twinkling and burning out. Thump Thump Thump Thump The massive lights switched on one by one, illuminating the entrance-ramp in a pattern of blue, yellow, and red. The colors by themselves were enough to give the fans their cue, and they were already booing in full-force when the music hit the PA system. “How could you abandon me, I thought you'd always be, There for me, standing tall, one and one for all, My support modeling, rolling as a team But instead on our own, enemies of old” BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM As the chorus hit, a cavalcade of fireworks exploded up from the stage, once again a mix of blue, yellow and red. As the smoke cleared, a spotlight illuminated the intended target of the crowd’s jeers, and thus, the decibel of the boos increased ten-fold. Guess who? “Season's change, the fallen rain will wash the pain away, Make amends to help understand how to live today,” Billows stood in the entrance-way with his head hung, cloaked in his custom-colored, lion's paw emblazoned robe. They hated him, and he loved that. And by the end of the night, they would find themselves hating him even more. Tonight, he would END their little hero's career, he would rid ACW of Midnight Cowboy. Tonight, The Risen Lion would prove himself king of the ACW jungle. This was the Key of Keystone State, the Reincarnation. Billows slowly raised his head, wearing a smile that told the entire story. It spoke volumes about just how much he enjoyed the fans’ hatred. Every single fan in that arena could feel right then and there that Billows held a true grudge against them and their displeasure was his treasure. It was maddening for them. Rather abruptly, after several seconds of not moving any other muscle sans her ocular ones had passed, Paige folded up the note and placed it bag in her gym bag. She took a deep breath and fussed about with her hair once more. It was something to keep her distracted. She needed it at that moment, you see. Once done, the hottest referee in ACW history was ready to go out there. "I'll see you on the other side, Chris." What was going on? We wouldn't find out now. ... But it HAD to be heavy. FOUR WAYS TO GLORY - ACW TAG TEAM TITLES to be announced vs. Abilities vs. the cabal vs. The Night Life © REFEREE: TOMAZ HEINREICH Many weeks ago, SlySports Entertainment LLC realized the ACW tag team division was in full swing and so to bring out the best in ratings and action, created a series of matches encased in a quasi-tournament: the Four Ways To Glory. Eight teams competed for spots in a coveted tournament to not only crown ACW Tag Team Champions, but to also award the winning team a $100K cash prize. COURAGE 127 saw Cabalites Jack Harris and Kesavan defeat the team of Big E. Smalls in a Glass Tables match. 128 saw Mach 2 given a second chance, only to have fallen into a trap by Jeremy Hunt and GFS, seeing their own Abilities team win. On 129, The Payne Gang scored a victory over Irish Spring and PowerTrip in a Singapore Cane match and on 130, defending champions The Night Life BARELY won out over a one-night return by the Heirs Of Wrestling, the inaugural and longest-reigning champions of the latest ACW era. The fifth edition of WORLD.WIDE.WRESTLECIDE saw a heinous assault by Abilities on the Payne Gang with a lead pipe. Their injuries were considered too serious to compete and their very future with ACW was in doubt, so a replacement team was chosen by ACW officials to fill the vacant spot. And now, Tommy Vale was going to be making the announcements as the fans came to life. At ringside, two men in suits were holding onto what appeared to be a bag of money, holding it up for all to see. “The following contest is the Four Ways To Glory Match! The rules are as follows… This is an elimination match with different stipulations for each fall. The first fall will be contested under regular tag team rules. The second match will be a Scorpion Rules Tag Team match. And the last match will take place within the confines of a steel cage! The last remaining team in the Four Ways to Glory will be the ACW Tag Team Champions and win the $100,000 cash prize!” Now? It was time to meet the participants. “People Hate Me” by The Murderdolls. The first team up in the match? Well, it was none other than two of the Huntsmen themselves! Two men who lived to serve the bidding of Jeremy Hunt, these two men won a qualifying match that was more or less a blatant set-up by Jeremy Hunt to get into this match. “First, making their way to the ring… representing the Huntsmen… Weighing in at 520 pounds… they are the team of Abraham Swift and William T. Rex… ABILITIES!” Oh, yeah, the fans didn’t like them at all, doing the bidding of Jeremy Hunt and being a pair of overall pricks. Big T. Rex flanked Abe Swift, who was talking his usual trash to anybody who would hear him. “Just give us that fuckin’ prize right now! Ain’t no way none of these other cunts are winning this! Then my sweet Elixr will be mine!” …Yeah. So they entered the ring, meanmugging the crowd before taking their place in their corner. The team of Abilities had come into their own over the last several months, but would really be the biggest win of their careers if they won the ACW Tag Team Titles tonight. “Still Running” by Chevelle. The crowd turned their head to the entryway next to see who the next tag team would be. The new music gave way to none other than the giants of the cabal. Jack Harris stood 6’9” and close to 300 pounds, but Kesavan was an even BIGGER force, standing at 7’ even and weighing just a bit north of 400 pounds. He was relatively new to the circle, but had left some painful impressions on members of the ACW roster already. Joe Bishop has announced a replacement for the team…” GROUND CONTROL, WE HAVE LIFT-OFF IN FIVE… FOUR… THREE… TWO… ONE… “Here Comes the Boom” by Nelly. The smirks on the face of Abilities? Dropped. Goodbye (For Good?) “I just don’t know what it’s going to take to get this going for you, Oso! And if it’s not going for you, it’s not going for me and that is a problem if I’ve ever heard one! I plotted for weeks on your behalf with drugged up, likely diseased scum and served you a title shot on a silver platter at your very first Pay Per View event! All you had to do was win! Am I supposed to do that too?!” There was a tone of seriousness sewn into the usual boom of Chester B. Wallace’s voice, normally as jovial as it was Southern, that rarely came out of the ever prized and often used mouth that rested upon his gelatinous face. Long ago, he had decided to project the image of a man who always saw the glass as being half full even if deep down he knew it was growing empty. It was better for the clients. He was a notorious bluffer, but convinced himself time and time again that it was for the greater good. “I’m only one man, Bear. And like it or not, this is a two man show. You knew it from jump street and still signed off on everything until the moment we debuted! I’ve lived up to my end of the bargain, but you haven’t been living up to yours!” He paced the dimly lit, shoddily decorated dressing room back and forth, breathing heavily. Oso Bannon sat on a bench behind where the agent walked, head buried in his hands. He was certainly the type to take a loss badly, but the duffle bag that was his constant companion anywhere he traveled laid open at his feet, suggesting that it might not just be the weight of defeat that was holding his head down. Chester Wallace’s face turned tomato red with rage. If it was Bugs Bunny instead of Oso Bannon provoking his anger, steam might have shot right out of his ears. “LOOK AT ME, GOD DAMN IT!” Oso slowly relented, but not without a visible struggle. Chester spat out a laugh that was completely devoid of any and all happiness, a laugh of complete disgust. He turned his back to “The Bear” for a moment and took in a deep drag of air. Rubbing his forehead, his face turned back to its normal pale white and he pivoted around to face his client once more, his thoughts collected and his mind seemingly, suddenly eased. “Listen, Oso... you didn’t have to do this. Any of it. You could have stayed back in Ohio, either serving time or serving at a restaurant. You can resent me and act like I’m just an opportunist who’s using you and your name to relaunch my own career and that might be one hundred percent true, but you can’t hide from the fact that I pulled the strings that have kept you free and your lifestyle in tact. The limousine rides, the hotel suites, the pills in that bag that you think I don’t notice, and those fucking headphones that are always in your ears... all bankrolled by me and a deal with ACW that you would have never been able to make on your own. You can say whatever you’d like about me, just as a lot of other people do. The harsh reality is that while I created a use for a partnership between us, I would still be alright without you. Especially since that partnership is only useful if you’re a winner. Do you remember what it feels like to be a winner? I don’t think you do.” Oso’s eyes met Chester’s for the first time in this particular encounter and possibly in weeks. The Bear’s big brown eyes looked scared and sad while the agent’s glared strongly. He was thinking of all the words he should say in defense or even just in response, but at the same time was getting lost in thoughts that “Ari Gold’s Worst Nightmare” had prompted within his always wandering brain. Once upon a time, Oso Bannon was consumed by winning, by being the best. He worked tirelessly and in turn became what he had dreamt of always becoming. He had survived a childhood in an awful Mexican ghetto and also the adolescent perils of being adopted, moving to America, and entering into the public school system. He had never even seen a football and was standing on the cusp of being one of the biggest sensations the game had ever witnessed. He could have had it all or as close to it as a human could get, but his desire to be the best eventually transformed into an ugly obsession with being better than the best. And that, kids, was his downfall. When his downward spiral finally ended, landing him in All-Star Championship Wrestling, he hoped that he would find his passion for competiton once more, but it proved a much more momentous task than he anticipated. There were flirtatious moments with former glory, but nothing that alluded to a true return to form. And there was the drugs... maybe different ones now, but still the same sick addiction. THWHAP! Chester Wallace’s constant companion, a plantation era wicker cane, came across Oso Bannon’s trademark nodding, wandering head. A small blotch of red appeared from within one of the many chalk white, fresh bandages that masked parts of his face that Tyson XL had earlier damaged. Just as swiftly as it had done its damage, the cane returned to Chester B’s side. “Do I have your attention now?” Wallace said calmly. Oso leaned his head back against the lockers, face wincing in pain. Through eyes blurred and barely opened, “The Bear” watched as his managed picked up his briefcase and grabbed for the handle of his carry on sized suitcase, back completely turned to his client as he headed towards the door. Twisting the handle, he walked out halfway, but then stuck his head back in. He had forgotten something, something that no great orator could afford to... a closing statement. “If you want to get high and daydream all day, it’s no skin of my pale ass, Oso. You’ll just have to find a way to do it without it being on my time or with my money. I’ve got to fly back to Los Angeles for an important meeting. Yes, in fact, it may come as news to you, but I’ve got other business ventures aside from you and they thankfully are faring much better. I’ll be texting details of your flight to Mexico to meet up with the rest of ACW within the week, but as of right now it’s up in the air as to whether or not I’ll be joining you. I’m sure you’ll be fine.” His problems with Chester B. Wallace could have filled a list a mile long, but Oso Bannon had always found comfort somewhere deep inside himself that he had a man like him on his side. But just like that, with one slam of a door, he was gone... ...And to think, there was still the End Game Match to look forward to. GRUDGE MATCH - STANDARD RULES Carrachio Salfuego vs. 'The Two' Booster Reid REFEREE: PAIGE ALLEN Carrachio Salfuego. 'The Two' Booster Reid. Six years from now, these two men could very well be doing battle in the ring for the ACW World Title. On this night, both men were to lock horns at the tail-end of their formative months in the industry. But if you asked them, both men would say that this match was extremely important. This would be their second singles encounter. It was during their first clash, way back on COURAGE 126, that this whole affair began. What transpired since then was mirred in controversy and a lot of other things. Along the way, both Carrachio and Booster found themselves surrending a chance to qualify for the 2010 End Game Match. Their little rivalry had gotten personal. And it reached a boiling point on COURAGE 130, with a tempremental interview. One man wanted to prove to not only his adopted (and legendary) father but to the world that he deserved to be what he was. The other man didn't want to let his words dominate his personality like so many others in the industry have; he wanted to let his in-ring ability shine through. He wanted to rise to the top. At the end of the day, this was a rivalry born out of competition and the will to break through to the next level. On a pay-per-view with matches that had plenty at stake, many outsiders decided to pass on talking about this match at length. Two youngsters doing battle to apease their competitive streaks? Yeah, it didn't connect with them. But it didn't matter to Carrachio or Booster. Whoever won would first and foremost earn the other's undeniable respect and understanding. Theme songs and entrances weren't a factor here. Both men heard their names introduced by Tommy Vale and went about their normal routines. It felt secondary to them. Within moments, both men blinked and found themselves in the ring, ready to put their honour and their names on the line in search for victory. To many, it seemed so ridiculous what these two were fighting about. To Booster Reid and Carrachio Salfuego, this match was to be the launching pad of their careers. The one who won would surely have this night to look back on. The one who lost would look back ruefully in a few months and wonder where he went wrong. Having checked both men, referee Paige Allen took a breath. She didn't have a personal interest in this match. Heck, she was distracted by something else entirely. Didn't mean she didn't notice the look of determination in Booster's eyes, or the glaze of intent in Carrachio's. This match, in a few years, would be a talking point. She just knew it. DING DING DING! A hush fell over the crowd, only bubbling in a few bursts of excitement at this clash of two youngsters. Quite possibly two men who would go on to become the future of ACW, some would say. The two competitors circled around the ring, staring intensely at each other. Both men made a few jerk movements, trying to lure the other off balance, but each time failed to resort in even a small twitch. They were highly-trained athletes looking for their openings, but trained in never revealing one of their own. Booster Reid lifted his head up, abandoning his forward hunch, and held one hand over his head. He appeared to be signaling for a test of strength with the Madrid Boy. Probably something his legendary father taught him. Carrachio stepped back, looking around at the fans to see what their thoughts were. A loud roar of approval came from those in attendances. Rising up from his own stance, Salfuego unwillingly fell right into The Two’s trap. Shooting in fast, Booster looked to aim at the erect legs of his adversary, hoping to take him down with a double leg takedown. SMACK! The fans fell silent for a moment, and then rose up in approving cheers. Booster Reid still had yet to realise just what had happened; the shooting pain paralysing his face hadn't quite resonated with the nerve centers in his brain. Carrachio Salfuego stepped back, removing his knee from Booster's face. Granted, Carrachio wore knee-pads but it was still a pretty sweet knee strike. With Booster standing stunned, referee Paige Allen stepped in to check that The Two's nose wasn't broken. It wasn't, which was all the signal Salfuego needed to strike. Arm-drag! Booster went flying across the ring. Having been snapped out of his trance, Booster scrambled back to his feet and charged back at Salfuego, but the Madrid Boy was relishing being involved in a match on a big pay-per-view. To be fair, Carrachio *did* have a match at IN ACROPOLIS 2010 but that ended up being gate-crashed by Arkady Rasputin and Aria Murphy. The Madrid Maniac also did feature at KING OF AGES 2010, losing to Chris Storm who had since disappeared. Where did he go? Hmm. What? Oh, the match. Anyways, yes; Carrachio was feeling invincible and easily made Booster taste canvas again via a solid hiptoss. So far, so not good for The Two. Once more, Booster scrambled back to his feet. Unfortunately for him, Carrachio Salfuego wasn't about to let his advantage slip. He quickly moved in and took down Booster with *his* double leg takedown, before flipping his body over and holding on to Booster's body. Immediately, referee Paige Allen hit the deck. Unique cover from Carrachio; ONE! TWO! T -- SHOULDER! Booster powered out, forcing his shoulder up from the canvas with authority. Before he could get up, though, Carrachio Salfuego shot himself into the ropes and smashed his shin into Booster's face, ensuring that The Two would remained plastered to the canvas. From there? Why, the Madrid Maniac began stomping away at the back of his adversary's head. Each blow fell like a hammer being slammed down onto his cranium, excerberated by the fans cheering Salfuego every step of the way. When Carrachio finally ceased with the stompity stomp, he opted to pull Booster Reid up. The Two went for a rogue swing that The Madrid Boy easily ducked, and followed up with a snap spinebuster takedown slam. The cover that came thereafter? It was tight, and it had the fans on their feet; ONE! TWO! T -- NO! Booster Reid was able to stay alive, and bitterly rolled towards the ropes. He needed a respite. A moment to collect his thoughts. Carrachio Salfuego wouldn't allow that, and was quickly on Booster again. The Madrid Boy grabbed a handful of Booster's hair and dragged him up, drawing a retaliatory elbow strike to Salfuego's gut. Carrachio fought through the pain and yanked Booster towards him, trapping him in a standing side-headlock. The Two instantly smiled. That made referee Paige Allen frown. Why would he... oh. That's why. Belly-to-back suplex! Great counter by Jimmy Reid's adopted heir. Of course, most of the fans didn't seem to think so. But Booster wasn't trying to impress them. He was trying to win a match, for himself. And for the victory he went; ONE! TWO! TH -- NO! There was still a lot of fight left in Carrachio. Booster shook his head and went right back to work, rolling the Madrid Boy onto his gut. Locking his hands tightly around Carrachio’s waist, Booster looked to try and wear his opponent down some before trying to capitalise on any further offense. Carrachio tried to escape, to break free, but no matter how he turned or bucked... Boooster Reid would not budge from his position on top of him. Hell, The Two would even resort to throwing down clubbing forearms onto Salfuego's upper spinal area to break down his defenses. That's why The Madrid Maniac figured it would be wise to consider another plan. One that involved reaching out for the nearby ropes, and grabbing hold of the bottom cable. Smart move, Carrachio. Instantly, referee Paige Allen stepped in and ordered Booster to get offa Carrachio. Booster relented, but only doing so after a quick flurry of stiff forearms to the back of the neck for good measure. The Two couldn't simply allow Salfuego to just get back up, after all. Standing up, 'The Two' Booster Reid endured a bit of admonishment from the red-headed referee before brushing past her and driving a boot up into the side of Carrachio's ribs. Instead of following up immediately, 'The Two' Booster Reid stepped back and allowed his adversary to get some air. When Salfuego was finally upright, Booster moved in again; he looked to try to score with a headlock, but The Madrid Boy ducked under his arm and shoved him into the ropes, catching him with his own headlock when Carrachio came back from aforementioned ropes. Wrenching away on the head, Carrachio Salfuego seemed to have regain the momentum in the match, a feat further proved when Booster Reid scrambled to the ropes to get the hold broken. Rather than allowing him to easily get away, Carrachio answered by releasing the headlock and catching The Two with a classic European Uppercut! That was instantly followed by a slap in the chest by way of an overhand chop to the chest. Booster clutched his chest, which had already turned red... but before the Madrid Boy could land another overhand chop, The Two lashed out with a pushing kick down onto Carrachio's right thigh. Thereafter, 'The Two' Booster Reid swiftly slapped on a front facelock and administered a vicious snap suplex! Booster quickly transitioned into a cover, making sure to hook both the legs; ONE! TWO! TH -- NO! Again, Carrachio proved that he wasn't going to go down so easily. Booster Reid shot to his feet and take to take a deep breath to stop himself from losing his cool. This current moment in time the biggest in his young career thus far. The Two could ill afford to let a moment of folly pave the way for his downfall. Measuring himself against the ropes, Booster Reid went for an elbow drop down onto the prone lifeform of Carrachio Salfuego. The Madrid Boy knew what was coming though, and rolled away in the nick of time! Booster's elbow spiked off the canvas, pain shooting up his entire arm. Carrachio Salfuego took advantage pof that, grabbing Reid's arm and using it to pull both of them up to a standing position. From there, Carrachio attempted yet another arm-drag. Blocked by Booster, who then surprised Carrachio with an arm-drag of his own! Salfuego crashed down to the canvas, and let the momentum of the arm-drag guide him out of the ring. The Madrid Boy needed space to regroup and refocus. Smirking, Booster Reid decided to follow his adversary outside. That would prove to be a rather silly move, because it was a TRAP~! Carrachio surpised The Two with a spinning heel kick to the sternum, drawing a large round of cheers! Carrachio grinned, feeling good about asserting his dominance over his rival. That happy feeling evaporated when Booster Reid extended his legs and tangled 'em with Salfuego's ankles, bringing the Madrid Boy down to the ground. In the ring, referee Paige Allen was halfway through her count. The fans got a little concerned, mainly because they were really keen on seeing a resolution to this rivalry. Carrachio and Booster quickly rushed to their feet and after shaking off their respective cobwebs, rolled back into the ring. With the fans applauding them for answering the ring-out count, both men locked eyes with each other. This was as close to a reset as they would get. Nodding each other, both men moved in to lock-up with each other. They appeared evenly matched, and the fans watched on as the attempt to tie-up continued for several more seconds. After much jockeying for position, Carrachio Salfuego was finally able to triumph and trap 'The Two' Booster Reid in a side headlock. Again. Booster immediately complained about 'extreme hair pullage', but referee Paige Allen wasn't buying any of that bullshit. With a wry grin, Carrachio took advantage of Paige's distracted state to grind his forearm across Booster's forehead. The Two took offense to that and retaliated by shoving Salfuego into the ropes. With the fans' cheers feeding him adrenaline, The Madrid Boy went off into the ropes again. This time, Booster Reid had a plan and turned over onto his gut, forcing Carrachio to leap over him upon his return from the ropes. Salfuego went on to the opposite set of ropes, and upon the second return was acrobatically leapfrogged by Booster Reid. Carrachio slapped on the brakes and swivelled on his feet, crouching and waiting for Booster Reid to turn around. Once he did just that? POWERSLAM BY CARRACHIO! The Madrid Boy gasped for air, and quickly hooked the legs. Paige dropped to her knees; ONE! TWO! THR -- NO! Almost had it, but almost doesn't count for anything in ACW. Rolling back up to a vertical position, Carrachio took hold of Booster's left arm and wrenched it sideways, forcing The Two to stagger up. Feeling confident of being in cruise control, Carrachio went on to whip Booster Reid towards the corner turnbuckle. Except, Booster countered and sent Salfuego flying over his head in a sweet monkey flip throw! Thereafter, Booster kipped up to his feet and spun around, extending his arms wide open. Carrachio stumbled back up to his feet and turned around, surprised at how Booster turned the momentum around. He would be further surprised when the heir to Jimmy Reid's legacy wrapped his arms around him. OVERHEAD BELLY TO BELLY SUPLEX! Performed to perfection! For that, Booster got some cheers. It didn't matter to him just then. The Two wanted the cheers to come after securing victory, which he hoped he had after crawling over to hook the legs; ONE! TWO! THR -- NO! Having squirmed out of the cover, Carrachio wisely rolled over to the ropes and manuevered himself into a seating position. That made referee Paige Allen step in between The Madrid Boy and Booster Reid, signalling to Booster that Carrachio had a hand on the ropes. The Two stepped back, happy to oblige. The nanosecond Carrachio removed his hand from the bottom cable? Booster out the pedal to the metal and exploded with a sliding dropkick to the lower base of Salfuego's spine! And that, lemme tell you, hurt like a mother-something. Showing that he was truly the student of Jimmy Reid, Booster dragged Carrachio away from the ropes. After which, he busted out a standing moonsault. It was rather sloppily executed, but his entire body weight came crashing down onto Carrachio. Sloppy or not, it still accomplished its purpose. Hooking the legs, Booster looked up at referee Paige Allen and hoped for the best; ONE! TWO! THR -- NO! Booster got to his knees and flared his nostrils. He thought he had it. Instead of arguing with the red-headed zerba about the count, Booster opted to pull Carrachio up to a vertical base and rather poetically slapped on a side headlock. Tightening it, Booster then dragged the Madrid Boy over to the ropes and walked along them, sliding and rubbing Carrachio's face against the top cable along the way. Rope burn? Ouch. Not being one to give up, he hopped to his feet with The Two's left leg tightly gripped underneath his armpit. Eww. Booster Reid clawed his way back up to his feet, stumbling around in a circle for a bit before Salfuego spun him around. Both men took a moment to get back up, the exertions of the match so far having taken its toll. Carrachio was the first up to his feet and swung a right hook at Booster Reid. Despite being rather woozy, The Two was alert enough to parry the hook and suddenly lashed out with a neat crescent kick! Salfuego stumbled backwards and eventually tripped over his own retreating feet, crashing down to the canvas. That suited 'The Two' Booster Reid just fine, for he had a plan. Booster stepped through the ropes and adjusted himself onto the apron, noting the reduction in jeers for him. The fans were just enthralled by this see-saw clash of the potential future All-Stars. Nodding to himself, Booster Reid grasped the top cable... slingshotted himself up and over in a sweet Guerrero-esque senton splash down onto Carrachio's sternum. Yeah, that was immensely badass. The fans thought so, giving Booster his due credit in the form of resounding cheers! Booster grinned and quickly darted on top of his rival, hooking the near leg and wishing to the stars that he had it this time. For real. In a flash, referee Paige Allen got to her knees and counted; ONE! TWO! THRE -- NO! SHOULDER! Booster Reid cursed silently as he got up. He knew that Carrachio Salfuego had garnered a reputation for being stubbornly tenacious but he was getting frustrated. Nonetheless, Booster pulled The Madrid Boy up and piled on the pressure, via a harsh snap-mare takedown. Almost immediately afterwards did Reid complete his tribute to one of his heroes (Curt Hennig!) in the form of a rolling neck-snap. Did Booster go for the cover again? You bet he did. Paige was on hand to count; ONE! TWO! TH -- NO! Did Carrachio kick out again? Affirmative. Booster Reid stood to his feet and ran his hands through his hair, holding back his tongue. A full 20 seconds passed before The Two yanked Carrachio up to his feet, at a near loss on what to do next. After a barrage of right hooks, The Two grabbed hold of The Madrid Boy's right arm and whipped him towards the corner turnbuckle. Or at least, that was the plan. At the last possible second, Carrachio slapped on the brakes and reversed the whip. The result saw Booster Reid collide face-first with the corner turnbuckle! The laws of whiplash effect saw Booster stagger back, completely vulnerable. Sensing that the stars were aligned in his favour, The Madrid Maniac took to rolling across the ring and knocked Booster back into the corner with a fierce clothesline. After which? The fans got to their feet. Carrachio had leapfrogged over Booster's motionless body and adjusted himself at the top of the turnbuckle. This was it. This was Salfuego's big moment, and there was nothing that could stop him from pulling off his deadly finisher. EL ESPIRITU DE MADRID! ... DE-NIED. Dropping down to the canvas to execute the last part of his explosive tornado DDT finisher, 'The Two' Booster Reid somehow dug down deep enough to -- rather spectacularly -- counter with a northern lights suplex. One that sent Carrachio Salfuego crashing into the corner turnbuckle! Needless to say, referee Paige Allen stepped back and grimaced. It looked like a scene out of a action move involving a train being derailed. In short: it wasn't pretty. Booster himself was wide-eyed, more shocked than anybody that he'd escaped being drilled by Carrachio's finisher and even went on to counter it. Standing to his feet, Booster felt an electric charge trickle down his spine. It was a sign to him. Slapping his right elbow, Booster turned around and crouched down, waiting for Carrachio Salfuego to pick himself up and stumble into his path. Finally, after about 12 seconds of waiting, The Madrid Maniac unwittingly acquiesced to The Two's wishful desire. IN YOUR FACE! For the first time in his career, 'The Two' Booster Reid had an opportunity to lay out somebody with his finishing move. And to his own delight, Booster pulled it off without a hitch. The cover was part of the package, and the fans held their breath; ONE! TWO! THREE! ... Ain't no joke, kiddos. Nas's Hero blared over the speakers. There was no doubt about it. It had taken Booster months, but he'd done it. He'd claimed his first victory, and on pay-per-view no less. Rolling away from Carrachio, The Two lightly smacked his own face. He was in disbelief. Then he looked up at the fans packed into the Stadio Olimpico, and a wave of something washed right over him. The fans weren't jeering. They were applauding. Cheering. Well, there were some that were furious, but by and large, 'The Two' Booster Reid was getting a positive ovation. Using the ropes to pull himself up, the realisation began to sunk in for Booster. Behind him, Carrachio Salfuego had recovered. He was not happy at the fact he'd lost... but yet, the Madrid Boy was smiling. Weird? Not at all, if you knew what was on his mind. "Hey. Booster." Reid peered over his shoulder, still needing the ropes to prop himself up. With the inside of his mouth going dry, The Two opened his mouth: "Yeah?" "Just wanted to say one thing." Carrachio began, shuffling closer to Booster Reid. "Throughout this match, we threw everything we had at each other. At the end of it, it boiled down to one simple fact. You were hungrier. You wanted it more. Hence, you win. Congratulations. You... did well." And then, Carrachio patted Booster on the shoulder. Booster grinned and nodded. By the time he'd thought up of a response, Salfuego had rolled out of the ring and took comfort in the fact that the fans in the front rows were giving him encouragement for a wonderful effort. 'The Two' Booster Reid looked around the squared circle and breathed deeply. With referee Paige Allen also choosing to take her leave, the ring was vacant. It was only Booster left in the ring. Strangely, he felt comforted most of all by that fact. Carrachio's words resonated with him, though. The cheers of the fans going up a few decibels? It made his heart swell. Finally!, Booster Reid thought to himself. He had arrived. Somewhere backstage, 'The One' Jimmy Reid watched his son soak in the moment of victory. The former ACW World Champion couldn't help but nod his head. "Finally." Jimmy muttered to himself. "He's finally ready. Now, I can bring him into the cabal." Uh oh. WINNER: 'The Two' Booster Reid via PINFALL. life as an official Cabalite. oh boy. Many people had walked through the doors of the Stadio Olimpico on this night. Some of them were contracted to ACW for a while. Some had just signed exclusive deals a couple of weeks ago. Some were just in for one night only. Y'know, for the End Game Match? Yeah. And then there was one particular man that had just dragged his feet through the doors of this historic building. One man who had seen his life turn around in the worst way possible over the last several weeks. Cap it off with news of SilverHAWK still being in a hospital after being punted in the head? Yeah, Trent Savage was not doing too well. Ever since losing in a bet to Z, Trent had seen himself drafted into the cabal. The funny thing was, ol' TS spent all of two days with the Caped Crusader and his merry gang before being set free. Trent walked down the hallway towards his assigned locker-room for the night, aware of the knowledge that the crucial GoldenHAWK/GFS showdown was about to come up next. Trent was also bracing himself to referee his only match of the night: Keller versus Zimmerman for the World Title. Savage had received no instructions from anybody else other than Joe Bishop since COURAGE 130, and with Bishop now also on the injury list thanks to Andy Sharp... the young zebra felt he was in no man's land. Heroes were falling, and the villains were slowly rising to power. "So I guess I wait until it's time." Trent quipped to himself. Sighing, Trent wondered silently to himself if anybody from the cabal was going to approach him tonight for some dastardly deed, since there was chatter of it. All Trent could hope for was for GoldenHAWK to win the impending match-up, and for Z's gang to leave him alone. Him being a Cabalite and not even getting to see his friend Pete Strombo was torture. Reaching his assigned abode for the night, Trent pushed the door open and poked his head in. Nothing, nobody waiting for him. Another sigh; this one was dripping with scant relief. He could rest in peace, for now, and join everybody else in watching GoldenHAWK fight for ACW's survival. Right, then. Let's get to that, shall we? Ready? "You ready for battle?" The unexpected voice caused GoldenHAWK to turn his attention towards the locker room door and away from the medical tape that he was wrapping his wrists in. Before him stood the man who just a week earlier had assisted in protecting him from being removed from wrestling all together let alone tonight's event. "You better believe it, Ark," G-Hawk responded with a smile and focused back on his taping. "I hope so," Arkady replied and pulled up a chair in front of the Golden Child. "To say that a lot of people are counting on you would be an understatement." "Don't I know it." "While I don't care who runs ACW, be it that bastard Hunt or your father, who I don't exactly see eye to eye with, I do care about the villains in this place trying to injure another fighter, putting them on the shelf before their time. With people like Denton, Billows, and Z running around, this place is a dangerous place for people who uphold truth, justice, and honor. Trust me when I say I understand your need for heroism, but I don't need to remind you of how much of a threat you pose to their way of life. You win tonight and your father will have the power to smite them all. They know it just as well as we do. That's why we need to make sure we don't have another attempt on your well-being." "What are you saying?" Arkady had managed to get G-Hawk's full focus. "I'm saying you give me the word and the Pack will be there." GoldenHAWK patted Arkady on the back with a smile. "I appreciate it, but not tonight." "Don't let your need to prove yourself cloud your judgement. You will have a long career in this business, if and only if you use your brain. They're not concerned with the thrill of battle or getting the victory in the game of human chess. They will sacrifice pawns to bring your defeat and have no issues with injuring you. You need support that you know will help you do what you aim to do. Doing it yourself will risk all that you and your father hold dear." GoldenHAWK rose from his seat and looked to his friend. The Russian Wolf could see the seriousness in his eyes. "Like I said, I appreciate it, Ark. I really do. But you know I can't accept it. I gotta do this on my own. I gotta show that one man can stand up to them all and come out on top. This is more than just battle between good and evil. This is me fighting for my family's legacy. I want to make sure the company that flows through my veins will be there for my children when I'm old and gray." "So, do what you know is best and say you want our help." "Not tonight, my friend." ACW's Son had grown decades in the weeks since the attack on his father, and years since the Original Pulp Hero had put the chance to save ACW in his very hands. Arkady left his seat and took a deep breath, before starting towards the door. "I hope you're right. Just remember the offer is out there should you change your mind." THE BATTLE FOR ACW / SPIRIT OF ACW CHAMPIONSHIP - STANDARD RULES GoldenHAWK vs. God's Forgotten Son © REFEREE: LEON HURST There's a first time for everything. See, normally when there is this much at stake, the two men in the match have fought before. Even at LEGENDS I, when Alias fought Osyrus with control of ACW in the balance, the two men had faced weeks before. Even before that, the two men had a history in another federation. So when Alias defeated Osyrus, and the reign of Ethan Winters Sr. had been ended by not only the Original Pulp Hero but by the entire ACW Resistance, though there were shocks and surprises we all knew what was at stake and who we were putting it at stake with. Tonight, a member of that original ACW Resistance, SilverHAWK would be remembered more then any other. When he had a hand in tearing down the business of fear that Ethan Winters Sr. had established, the three-time ACW World Champion had never expected to one day control the day to day business of ACW as its General Manager. With men like ACW's original co-founders, Charles Dunn and James Boyd, as his mentors though... SilverHAWK made sure that ACW would not fall, and even when it did fall due to the guidance of others, he'd bring it back. He'd always return All-Star Championship Wrestling to life. And if Rome, Italy wasn't life, then you just weren't living. So it pained many people who had recently fought for ACW, especially SilverHAWK's son... the GoldenHAWK, that SilverHAWK wouldn't be here tonight. The ghosts of the past had come back to haunt SilverHAWK in the form of Ethan Winter Sr's nephew. Jeremy Hunt had established himself within the media conglomerate SlySports, and after months of establishing himself as a political power within ACW, struck when the time was right... and started taking over. Not only bringing members of ACW's roster to his side, like the infamous Khristain Keller, the one-time feel good story of Jesse Ramey and the most controversial man in ACW, Dean Denton... but also... God's Forgotten Son. The 2004 King Of Ages returned to ACW, and made one thing clear once he won the Spirit of ACW. The God of which he spoke? That man was his father, Ethan Winters Sr. And all those that led to his downfall and his untimely demise would pay dearly for it. SilverHAWK and ACW, most of all. Using the Spirit of ACW, God's Forgotten Son established a final battle between Jeremy Hunt and SilverHAWK. And then, on COURAGE 130 he made sure that SilverHAWK wouldn't even be able to witness that final battle when he punted him in the head and left him on the brink of death. In the path of vengeance for his father, God's Forgotten Son had made a dear enemy with the son of another man. Now tonight, at END GAME 2010, Aaron Jones Jr, GoldenHAWK would face off against Ethan Winters Jr, with all the motivation in the world to make his DEBUT in ACW. There's a first time for everything. GoldenHAWK's first time in an ACW ring? It was ALL on the line. If GoldenHAWK won, then Jeremy Hunt would be banned from ACW and Joe Bishop would no doubt become the interim General Manager until SilverHAWK returned as everyone within ACW would hope that he did. If God's Forgotten Son won, then SilverHAWK would be fired from ACW, as he lay in a hospital in Italy, still unconscious, as the clock struck midnight on the bright future of ACW and darkness reigned. The Spirit of ACW would go to the victor, but either one of legacy's son fought for SO much more. War Within A Breath by Rage Against The Machine GoldenHAWK, with words of encouragement from the likes of Sheffield and Arkady in his head, dove out from the back and almost flew down to the ring side area. His hands slapped those at ringside but his feet barely touched the ground. Fireworks of gold exploding in the background. Unflinching war roared over the PA system, music of his father's choosing, but GoldenHAWK could barely hear it. He could only hear the voices of the men and women who worked for ACW, the men and women who supported him in this fight tonight. Tonight, GoldenHAWK's feet barely touched the ground, tonight in Rome, G-HAWK flew. He would win tonight. He had to. It was good versus evil. When you walk through the garden you gotta watch your back well I beg your pardon walk the straight and narrow track… The roar of Tom Waits, accompanied by off-tune trumpets was still a disconcerting sound within the walls of ACW, even more so after what HE had done and with the jeers of the Italian crowd... well they knew it too, and it brought with it a disconcerting presence. The lights dimmed as God’s Forgotten Son walked to the ring in a single spotlight. His upper-body covered mostly by his custom hooded cloak. If you walk with Jesus he's gonna save your soul you gotta keep the devil way down in the hole… As he made it to the ring, he threw by his hood to reveal his emotionless visage, and after he removed the cloak and left it at ringside the entire arena could see the glimmering beauty of the Spirit of ACW as it clung around his waist. SINGLES MATCH - ACW WORLD TITLE Z vs. Keith Scott Zimmerman © REFEREE: TRENT SAVAGE Keith Scott Zimmerman clenched his fists. This wasn't going to be pretty. On one hand, he half-expected it. OH WAIT, ANDY SHARP WITH A KICK TO THE BALLS! And back down to a kneeling position Keith went. Mr. All-Star began to brace himself. He took four steps back and got in touch with his inner-Orton. What? That's a thing. With nothing stopping him, Andy Sharp went for the killer blow. THE SHINING EXAM--NOOOOOOOO! Christopher Sheffield to the rescue, yanking Keith Scott Zimmerman out of harm's way at the last possible second. Andy Sharp turned around and roared, furious. Sheffield wouldn't have any of it; kick to the gut, uppercut to the jaw, and a pick-up into a tilt-a-whirl. That's right. Sheffield was going for it. A-BOMB! Except, it wasn't. Andy Sharp had used the forces of momentum -- aided by a rogue knee to Sheffield's jaw while being held upside down -- to counter Sheffield's tilt-a-whirl piledriver into something more deadly. It was a new one for Mr. All-Star. Something he'd already administered once in the match, to Keith Scott Zimmerman no less. BLUNT FORCE TRAUMA! What of Keith, by the by? Ah, Sharp hadn't forgotten about him. He quickly rose up and turned around, catching the incoming Keith with a jumping kick to the sternum. Thereafter? Say, Keith? How about you HIT ANDY'S MUSIC?! Oh yeah, Z wasn't the only one ripping off Keith's finisher now! With two downed bodies in front of him, Sharp had to choose who to get rid of first. Tough? Not in the slightest. "This is for last year!" Yeah, he opted to pin Sheffield first. Let's see how that worked out; ONE! TWO! THREE! Pretty well, it seemed. ELIMINATION: Christopher Sheffield -- by Andy Sharp Two times in his ACW career, The Man Without An Alias would embrace the End Game Match as #30. And both times, Sheffield would not be able to walk out the winner. Still, at least he got a shot at the ACW Tag Team Titles out of it? It could sit pretty next to the shot at the Spirit Of ACW Title he'd earned months earlier, eh? Sharp didn't care. He scrambled across the ring and jumped on top of Keith. Was this the moment? The fans held their breath, as Irwonsen counted Sharp's cover of KSZ; ONE! TWO! THRE--NOOOOOOOOO~! Nope, it wasn't Sharp's moment of glory. Yet. Andy rolled away from Keith and put his hands on his hips. He was exhausted. So was KSZ. In some ways, Keith was worse off than him. Yet, Andy suddenly felt that he had a ton of weight on his shoulders. Perhaps it was Z, watching on intently. By this point, the Caped Crusader had climbed over the barricade and stood on top of the Pakistani Broadcast Booth, ACW World Championship clutched in his left hand. Shaking his head, Mr. All-Star stood up and motioned for Keith to do the same. Zimm in turn flashed Andy the ol' one-finger salute. Outraged, Andy stormed over to Keith. SUCKER PUNCH TO THE GUT! Keith then yanked on Sharp's tights and sent him sprawling into the corner turnbuckle. Sharp was able to prevent his face from making violent contact with the turnbuckle, but was unable to prevent getting smacked in the face by a rolling koppu kick. After that? Well, Andy Sharp was in trouble. Biiiiiiig trouble. BEST BRAINBUSTER IN THE BUSINESS! Let's take a poll: how awesome, on a scale of 1 to 10, was that? This'll just take a moment. ... Processing. Ah ha. The fans say: 348. Well. That's higher than 10 by a lot, soooo. Yay! With the crowd going batshit insane, Keith rolled on top of Sharp and hooked the right leg. Jimmy Reid blew a gasket, unable to fathom what was happening. ONE! TWO! THREEHANDONTHEROPE! Sharp had survived. Like a cockroach. In the distance, the sounds of non-wrestling fans ushering in the new year were heard by some. Z looked down from his perched position, at the clock that lay on the Pakistani Broadcast Table. It confirmed what he already knew. It was already 30 seconds into the brand new year. The Caped Crusader couldn't resist. He chuckled, and watched as Keith Scott Zimmerman rose to his feet, hobbling over to the corner turnbuckle. Andy Sharp slowly followed suit in getting vertical, brains scrambled. Unable to place where exactly he was. His bearings were completely shot to hell. That suited Keith just perfectly. He charged ahead, and roared when Sharp turned around. HIT MY MUSIC! Andy Sharp may have been able to kick out of KSZ's deadly brainbuster. But everybody and their grandmother knew that he wasn't going to kick out this time 'round. Keith didn't even bother hooking the legs. He just got an arm across Sharp's chest, and looked over his shoulder, at Z. At *his* World Championship. ONE. At the title he'd lost about an hour and twenty minutes earlier. TWO. At the man that made a fool out of him. THREE. At the man he would now meet... at LEGENDS V. The bell rang. The fans exploded. Henry Irwonsen silently did a 'hell yeah'. It was all over. END GAME 2010 had stretched into the opening moments of 2011, but at the end of it all, a winner was found. Quite possibly nobody's best bet to win, mainly because he wasn't supposed to even be in it. The man he'd bested at the death had to settle with a shot at the Spirit Of ACW Title. Because the winner, had a shot at the big gold belt. "LADIES AND GENTLEMEN! I INTRODUCE TO YOU, THE WINNER OF THE 2010 END GAME MATCH... KEEEEEITTTTTTTH! SCOTTTTTTTTTTTT! ZIMMMMMMMERMANNNNNNNNNNNN!" Tommy Vale announced, happy as all fuck. Jenna wept tears of joy. Renaud Cardinal stood up and clapped. Even though the reality of the situation was that Jeremy Hunt was now in charge. Even though Z -- locking eyes with Keith Scott Zimmerman again -- raised the ACW World Championship up above his head to affirm that he was undoubtedly the ACW World Champion. Even though the blackest night had arrived in ACW... there was but a glimmer of hope. That white light of hope was now standing on his two feet, sending a message to a man that'd broken his body but not his spirit. Keith Scott Zimmerman's face may have been caked in blood. He may have been seeing double of everything at that moment. But above all else, Keith was sure of one thing and one thing only. "This isn't over." Z bared his teeth and adjusted his mask. "It sure isn't." Ladies and gentlemen, this has been END GAME 2010. ... but this HASN'T been the end of the journey. Not quite there yet. See you in 2011, in Mexico. WINNER: Keith Scott Zimmerman via ULTIMATE HEROISM. F I N.
